

===== FILE: .\XianYuMarket\src\main\java\com\klei\goodfish\controller\AdminDeleteCommentServlet.java =====

package com.klei.goodfish.controller;

import com.google.gson.Gson;
import com.klei.goodfish.dto.AdminDeleteDTO;
import com.klei.goodfish.service.CommentService;
import com.klei.goodfish.service.impl.CommentServiceImpl;
import com.klei.goodfish.util.BusinessException;
import com.klei.goodfish.util.ResultUtil;

import javax.servlet.ServletException;
import javax.servlet.annotation.WebServlet;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpServlet;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpServletRequest;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpServletResponse;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpSession;
import java.io.BufferedReader;
import java.io.IOException;
import java.io.PrintWriter;

@WebServlet("/admin/comment/delete")
public class AdminDeleteCommentServlet extends HttpServlet {

    private CommentService commentService = new CommentServiceImpl();
    private Gson gson = new Gson();

    @Override
    protected void doPost(HttpServletRequest req, HttpServletResponse resp)
            throws ServletException, IOException {

        req.setCharacterEncoding("UTF-8");
        resp.setContentType("application/json;charset=UTF-8");
        PrintWriter out = resp.getWriter();

        try {
            HttpSession session = req.getSession();
            Integer userId = (Integer) session.getAttribute("userId");
            Integer role = (Integer) session.getAttribute("role");

            if (userId == null) {
                resp.setStatus(401);
                out.print(ResultUtil.fail(401, "璇峰厛鐧诲綍").toJson());
                return;
            }

            if (role == null || role != 1) {
                resp.setStatus(403);
                out.print(ResultUtil.fail(403, "鏃犳潈鎿嶄綔锛岄渶瑕佺鐞嗗憳鏉冮檺").toJson());
                return;
            }

            StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
            BufferedReader reader = req.getReader();
            String line;
            while ((line = reader.readLine()) != null) {
                sb.append(line);
            }

            AdminDeleteDTO dto = gson.fromJson(sb.toString(), AdminDeleteDTO.class);

            if (dto.getCommentId() == null) {
                resp.setStatus(400);
                out.print(ResultUtil.fail(400, "璇勮ID涓嶈兘涓虹┖").toJson());
                return;
            }

            boolean success = commentService.adminDeleteComment(dto.getCommentId());

            if (success) {
                out.print(ResultUtil.success("鍒犻櫎璇勮鎴愬姛", null).toJson());
            } else {
                resp.setStatus(500);
                out.print(ResultUtil.fail(500, "鍒犻櫎澶辫触锛岃瘎璁轰笉瀛樺湪").toJson());
            }

        } catch (BusinessException e) {
            resp.setStatus(e.getCode());
            out.print(ResultUtil.fail(e.getCode(), e.getMessage()).toJson());
        } catch (Exception e) {
            resp.setStatus(500);
            // 鈽呪槄鈽?鍏抽敭淇锛氫慨姝ｅ瓧绗︿覆鎷兼帴璇硶閿欒 鈽呪槄鈽?
            out.print(ResultUtil.fail(500, "绯荤粺閿欒锛? + e.getMessage()).toJson());
        }
    }
}



===== FILE: .\XianYuMarket\src\main\java\com\klei\goodfish\controller\AdminDeleteGoodServlet.java =====

package com.klei.goodfish.controller;

import com.google.gson.Gson;
import com.klei.goodfish.dto.AdminDeleteDTO;
import com.klei.goodfish.service.GoodService;
import com.klei.goodfish.service.impl.GoodServiceImpl;
import com.klei.goodfish.util.BusinessException;
import com.klei.goodfish.util.ResultUtil;

import javax.servlet.ServletException;
import javax.servlet.annotation.WebServlet;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpServlet;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpServletRequest;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpServletResponse;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpSession;
import java.io.BufferedReader;
import java.io.IOException;
import java.io.PrintWriter;

@WebServlet("/admin/good/delete")
public class AdminDeleteGoodServlet extends HttpServlet {

    private GoodService goodService = new GoodServiceImpl();
    private Gson gson = new Gson();

    @Override
    protected void doPost(HttpServletRequest req, HttpServletResponse resp)
            throws ServletException, IOException {

        req.setCharacterEncoding("UTF-8");
        resp.setContentType("application/json;charset=UTF-8");
        PrintWriter out = resp.getWriter();

        try {
            HttpSession session = req.getSession();
            Integer userId = (Integer) session.getAttribute("userId");
            Integer role = (Integer) session.getAttribute("role");

            // 妫€鏌ョ櫥褰?
            if (userId == null) {
                resp.setStatus(401);
                out.print(ResultUtil.fail(401, "璇峰厛鐧诲綍").toJson());
                return;
            }

            // 妫€鏌ョ鐞嗗憳鏉冮檺锛?=鏅€氱敤鎴凤紝1=绠＄悊鍛橈級
            if (role == null || role != 1) {
                resp.setStatus(403);
                out.print(ResultUtil.fail(403, "鏃犳潈鎿嶄綔锛岄渶瑕佺鐞嗗憳鏉冮檺").toJson());
                return;
            }

            // 璇诲彇 JSON
            StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
            BufferedReader reader = req.getReader();
            String line;
            while ((line = reader.readLine()) != null) {
                sb.append(line);
            }

            AdminDeleteDTO dto = gson.fromJson(sb.toString(), AdminDeleteDTO.class);

            if (dto.getGoodId() == null) {
                resp.setStatus(400);
                out.print(ResultUtil.fail(400, "鍟嗗搧ID涓嶈兘涓虹┖").toJson());
                return;
            }

            // 璋冪敤 Service锛堥€昏緫鍒犻櫎鎴栫墿鐞嗗垹闄わ級
            boolean success = goodService.adminDeleteGood(dto.getGoodId());

            if (success) {
                out.print(ResultUtil.success("鍒犻櫎鍟嗗搧鎴愬姛", null).toJson());
            } else {
                resp.setStatus(500);
                out.print(ResultUtil.fail(500, "鍒犻櫎澶辫触锛屽晢鍝佷笉瀛樺湪").toJson());
            }

        } catch (BusinessException e) {
            resp.setStatus(e.getCode());
            out.print(ResultUtil.fail(e.getCode(), e.getMessage()).toJson());
        } catch (Exception e) {
            resp.setStatus(500);
            // 鈽呪槄鈽?鍏抽敭淇锛氫慨姝ｅ瓧绗︿覆鎷兼帴璇硶閿欒 鈽呪槄鈽?
            out.print(ResultUtil.fail(500, "绯荤粺閿欒锛? + e.getMessage()).toJson());
        }
    }
}



===== FILE: .\XianYuMarket\src\main\java\com\klei\goodfish\controller\CommentDeleteServlet.java =====

package com.klei.goodfish.controller;

import com.google.gson.Gson;
import com.klei.goodfish.service.CommentService;
import com.klei.goodfish.service.impl.CommentServiceImpl;
import com.klei.goodfish.util.BusinessException;
import com.klei.goodfish.util.ResultUtil;

import javax.servlet.ServletException;
import javax.servlet.annotation.WebServlet;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpServlet;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpServletRequest;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpServletResponse;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpSession;
import java.io.BufferedReader;
import java.io.IOException;
import java.io.PrintWriter;

/**
 * 鍒犻櫎璇勮锛堥渶鐧诲綍锛屽彧鑳藉垹鑷繁鐨勬垨绠＄悊鍛橈級
 * POST /comment/delete
 * Body: {"commentId":1}
 * @author klei
 */
@WebServlet("/comment/delete")
public class CommentDeleteServlet extends HttpServlet {

    private CommentService commentService = new CommentServiceImpl();
    private Gson gson = new Gson();

    @Override
    protected void doPost(HttpServletRequest req, HttpServletResponse resp)
            throws ServletException, IOException {

        req.setCharacterEncoding("UTF-8");
        resp.setContentType("application/json;charset=UTF-8");
        PrintWriter out = resp.getWriter();

        try {
            HttpSession session = req.getSession();
            Integer userId = (Integer) session.getAttribute("userId");

            StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
            BufferedReader reader = req.getReader();
            String line;
            while ((line = reader.readLine()) != null) {
                sb.append(line);
            }

            // 绠€鍗曡В鏋?JSON 鑾峰彇 commentId
            String json = sb.toString();
            Integer commentId = null;
            if (json.contains("\"commentId\"")) {
                String value = json.replaceAll(".*\"commentId\"\\s*:\\s*(\\d+).*", "$1");
                commentId = Integer.parseInt(value);
            }

            if (commentId == null) {
                resp.setStatus(400);
                out.print(ResultUtil.fail(400, "璇勮ID涓嶈兘涓虹┖").toJson());
                return;
            }

            boolean success = commentService.deleteComment(commentId, userId);

            if (success) {
                out.print(ResultUtil.success("鍒犻櫎鎴愬姛", null).toJson());
            } else {
                resp.setStatus(500);
                out.print(ResultUtil.fail(500, "鍒犻櫎澶辫触").toJson());
            }

        } catch (BusinessException e) {
            resp.setStatus(e.getCode());
            out.print(ResultUtil.fail(e.getCode(), e.getMessage()).toJson());
        } catch (Exception e) {
            resp.setStatus(500);
            out.print(ResultUtil.fail(500, "鍒犻櫎澶辫触锛? + e.getMessage()).toJson());
        }
    }

    @Override
    protected void doGet(HttpServletRequest req, HttpServletResponse resp)
            throws ServletException, IOException {
        resp.setContentType("application/json;charset=UTF-8");
        resp.setStatus(405);
        resp.getWriter().print(ResultUtil.fail(405, "璇蜂娇鐢?POST 鏂规硶").toJson());
    }
}



===== FILE: .\XianYuMarket\src\main\java\com\klei\goodfish\controller\CommentLikeServlet.java =====

package com.klei.goodfish.controller;

import com.google.gson.Gson;
import com.klei.goodfish.dto.CommentLikeDTO;
import com.klei.goodfish.service.CommentLikeService;
import com.klei.goodfish.service.impl.CommentLikeServiceImpl;
import com.klei.goodfish.util.BusinessException;
import com.klei.goodfish.util.ResultUtil;

import javax.servlet.ServletException;
import javax.servlet.annotation.WebServlet;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpServlet;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpServletRequest;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpServletResponse;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpSession;
import java.io.BufferedReader;
import java.io.IOException;
import java.io.PrintWriter;

@WebServlet("/comment/like")
public class CommentLikeServlet extends HttpServlet {

    private CommentLikeService likeService = new CommentLikeServiceImpl();
    private Gson gson = new Gson();

    @Override
    protected void doPost(HttpServletRequest req, HttpServletResponse resp)
            throws ServletException, IOException {

        req.setCharacterEncoding("UTF-8");
        resp.setContentType("application/json;charset=UTF-8");
        PrintWriter out = resp.getWriter();

        try {
            HttpSession session = req.getSession();
            Integer userId = (Integer) session.getAttribute("userId");

            if (userId == null) {
                resp.setStatus(401);
                out.print(ResultUtil.fail(401, "璇峰厛鐧诲綍").toJson());
                return;
            }

            // 璇诲彇 JSON
            StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
            BufferedReader reader = req.getReader();
            String line;
            while ((line = reader.readLine()) != null) {
                sb.append(line);
            }

            // 瑙ｆ瀽鍙傛暟
            java.util.Map map = gson.fromJson(sb.toString(), java.util.Map.class);
            Object commentIdObj = map.get("commentId");

            if (commentIdObj == null) {
                resp.setStatus(400);
                out.print(ResultUtil.fail(400, "璇勮ID涓嶈兘涓虹┖").toJson());
                return;
            }

            Integer commentId = ((Number) commentIdObj).intValue();

            CommentLikeDTO dto = new CommentLikeDTO();
            dto.setCommentId(commentId);
            dto.setUserId(userId);

            boolean success = likeService.like(dto);

            if (success) {
                out.print(ResultUtil.success("鐐硅禐鎴愬姛", null).toJson());
            } else {
                resp.setStatus(500);
                out.print(ResultUtil.fail(500, "鐐硅禐澶辫触").toJson());
            }

        } catch (BusinessException e) {
            resp.setStatus(e.getCode());
            out.print(ResultUtil.fail(e.getCode(), e.getMessage()).toJson());
        } catch (Exception e) {
            resp.setStatus(500);
            out.print(ResultUtil.fail(500, "绯荤粺閿欒锛? + e.getMessage()).toJson());
        }
    }
}



===== FILE: .\XianYuMarket\src\main\java\com\klei\goodfish\controller\CommentListServlet.java =====

package com.klei.goodfish.controller;

import com.google.gson.Gson;
import com.klei.goodfish.service.CommentService;
import com.klei.goodfish.service.impl.CommentServiceImpl;
import com.klei.goodfish.util.BusinessException;
import com.klei.goodfish.util.ResultUtil;
import com.klei.goodfish.vo.CommentVO;

import javax.servlet.ServletException;
import javax.servlet.annotation.WebServlet;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpServlet;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpServletRequest;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpServletResponse;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpSession;
import java.io.IOException;
import java.io.PrintWriter;
import java.util.List;

@WebServlet("/comment/list")
public class CommentListServlet extends HttpServlet {

    private CommentService commentService = new CommentServiceImpl();
    private Gson gson = new Gson();

    @Override
    protected void doGet(HttpServletRequest req, HttpServletResponse resp)
            throws ServletException, IOException {

        req.setCharacterEncoding("UTF-8");
        resp.setContentType("application/json;charset=UTF-8");
        PrintWriter out = resp.getWriter();

        try {
            String goodIdStr = req.getParameter("goodId");
            System.out.println("[CommentList] 鏀跺埌璇锋眰锛実oodId鍙傛暟: " + goodIdStr);

            if (goodIdStr == null || goodIdStr.trim().isEmpty()) {
                resp.setStatus(400);
                out.print(ResultUtil.fail(400, "鍟嗗搧ID涓嶈兘涓虹┖").toJson());
                return;
            }

            Integer goodId = Integer.parseInt(goodIdStr);

            // 鑾峰彇褰撳墠鐢ㄦ埛ID锛堝彲鑳戒负null锛岃〃绀烘湭鐧诲綍锛?
            HttpSession session = req.getSession(false);
            Integer currentUserId = null;
            if (session != null) {
                currentUserId = (Integer) session.getAttribute("userId");
            }

            System.out.println("[CommentList] 鏌ヨ鍟嗗搧璇勮锛実oodId=" + goodId + ", currentUserId=" + currentUserId);

            // 浼犲叆currentUserId锛堝厑璁镐负null锛?
            List<CommentVO> list = commentService.getCommentsByGoodId(goodId, currentUserId);

            System.out.println("[CommentList] 鏌ヨ瀹屾垚锛岃繑鍥炵粰鍓嶇鐨勮瘎璁烘暟: " + (list != null ? list.size() : 0));

            // 鍏抽敭鏂板锛氳缁嗘棩蹇楋紝鎵撳嵃鍓?鏉¤瘎璁烘暟鎹?
            if (list != null && !list.isEmpty()) {
                for (int i = 0; i < Math.min(list.size(), 3); i++) {
                    CommentVO vo = list.get(i);
                    System.out.println("[CommentList] 璇勮 " + i + ": id=" + vo.getCommentId()
                            + ", userId=" + vo.getUserId()
                            + ", userName=" + vo.getUserName()
                            + ", content=" + (vo.getContent() != null ? vo.getContent().substring(0, Math.min(20, vo.getContent().length())) + "..." : "null"));
                }
            } else {
                System.out.println("[CommentList] 璀﹀憡锛氳繑鍥炵粰鍓嶇鐨勫垪琛ㄤ负绌猴紒");
            }

            out.print(ResultUtil.success("鏌ヨ鎴愬姛", list).toJson());

        } catch (NumberFormatException e) {
            System.err.println("[CommentList] 鍙傛暟鏍煎紡閿欒: " + e.getMessage());
            resp.setStatus(400);
            out.print(ResultUtil.fail(400, "鍟嗗搧ID鏍煎紡閿欒").toJson());
        } catch (BusinessException e) {
            System.err.println("[CommentList] 涓氬姟寮傚父: " + e.getMessage());
            resp.setStatus(e.getCode());
            out.print(ResultUtil.fail(e.getCode(), e.getMessage()).toJson());
        } catch (Exception e) {
            System.err.println("[CommentList] 绯荤粺寮傚父: " + e.getMessage());
            e.printStackTrace();
            resp.setStatus(500);
            out.print(ResultUtil.fail(500, "绯荤粺閿欒: " + e.getMessage()).toJson());
        }
    }

    @Override
    protected void doPost(HttpServletRequest req, HttpServletResponse resp)
            throws ServletException, IOException {
        resp.setContentType("application/json;charset=UTF-8");
        resp.setStatus(405);
        resp.getWriter().print(ResultUtil.fail(405, "璇蜂娇鐢℅ET鏂规硶").toJson());
    }
}



===== FILE: .\XianYuMarket\src\main\java\com\klei\goodfish\controller\CommentPublishServlet.java =====

package com.klei.goodfish.controller;

import com.google.gson.Gson;
import com.klei.goodfish.dto.CommentPublishDTO;
import com.klei.goodfish.service.CommentService;
import com.klei.goodfish.service.impl.CommentServiceImpl;
import com.klei.goodfish.util.BusinessException;
import com.klei.goodfish.util.ResultUtil;
import com.klei.goodfish.vo.CommentVO;

import javax.servlet.ServletException;
import javax.servlet.annotation.WebServlet;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpServlet;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpServletRequest;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpServletResponse;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpSession;
import java.io.BufferedReader;
import java.io.IOException;
import java.io.PrintWriter;

@WebServlet("/comment/publish")
public class CommentPublishServlet extends HttpServlet {

    private CommentService commentService = new CommentServiceImpl();
    private Gson gson = new Gson();

    @Override
    protected void doPost(HttpServletRequest req, HttpServletResponse resp)
            throws ServletException, IOException {

        req.setCharacterEncoding("UTF-8");
        resp.setContentType("application/json;charset=UTF-8");
        PrintWriter out = resp.getWriter();

        try {
            HttpSession session = req.getSession();
            Integer userId = (Integer) session.getAttribute("userId");

            if (userId == null) {
                resp.setStatus(401);
                out.print(ResultUtil.fail(401, "璇峰厛鐧诲綍").toJson());
                return;
            }

            StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
            BufferedReader reader = req.getReader();
            String line;
            while ((line = reader.readLine()) != null) {
                sb.append(line);
            }

            String requestBody = sb.toString();
            System.out.println("[CommentPublish] 鏀跺埌璇锋眰浣? " + requestBody);

            CommentPublishDTO dto = gson.fromJson(requestBody, CommentPublishDTO.class);
            dto.setUserId(userId);

            System.out.println("[CommentPublish] 鍙戝竷璇勮锛実oodId=" + dto.getGoodId() + ", userId=" + userId);

            CommentVO vo = commentService.publishComment(dto);

            System.out.println("[CommentPublish] 鍙戝竷鎴愬姛锛岃瘎璁篒D: " + (vo != null ? vo.getCommentId() : "null"));
            out.print(ResultUtil.success("璇勮鎴愬姛", vo).toJson());

        } catch (BusinessException e) {
            System.err.println("[CommentPublish] 涓氬姟寮傚父: " + e.getMessage());
            resp.setStatus(e.getCode());
            out.print(ResultUtil.fail(e.getCode(), e.getMessage()).toJson());
        } catch (Exception e) {
            System.err.println("[CommentPublish] 绯荤粺寮傚父: " + e.getMessage());
            e.printStackTrace();
            resp.setStatus(500);
            out.print(ResultUtil.fail(500, "璇勮澶辫触: " + e.getMessage()).toJson());
        }
    }

    @Override
    protected void doGet(HttpServletRequest req, HttpServletResponse resp)
            throws ServletException, IOException {
        resp.setContentType("application/json;charset=UTF-8");
        resp.setStatus(405);
        resp.getWriter().print(ResultUtil.fail(405, "璇蜂娇鐢≒OST鏂规硶").toJson());
    }
}



===== FILE: .\XianYuMarket\src\main\java\com\klei\goodfish\controller\CommentUnlikeServlet.java =====

package com.klei.goodfish.controller;

import com.google.gson.Gson;
import com.klei.goodfish.dto.CommentLikeDTO;
import com.klei.goodfish.service.CommentLikeService;
import com.klei.goodfish.service.impl.CommentLikeServiceImpl;
import com.klei.goodfish.util.BusinessException;
import com.klei.goodfish.util.ResultUtil;

import javax.servlet.ServletException;
import javax.servlet.annotation.WebServlet;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpServlet;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpServletRequest;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpServletResponse;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpSession;
import java.io.BufferedReader;
import java.io.IOException;
import java.io.PrintWriter;

@WebServlet("/comment/unlike")
public class CommentUnlikeServlet extends HttpServlet {

    private CommentLikeService likeService = new CommentLikeServiceImpl();
    private Gson gson = new Gson();

    @Override
    protected void doPost(HttpServletRequest req, HttpServletResponse resp)
            throws ServletException, IOException {

        req.setCharacterEncoding("UTF-8");
        resp.setContentType("application/json;charset=UTF-8");
        PrintWriter out = resp.getWriter();

        try {
            HttpSession session = req.getSession();
            Integer userId = (Integer) session.getAttribute("userId");

            if (userId == null) {
                resp.setStatus(401);
                out.print(ResultUtil.fail(401, "璇峰厛鐧诲綍").toJson());
                return;
            }

            // 璇诲彇 JSON
            StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
            BufferedReader reader = req.getReader();
            String line;
            while ((line = reader.readLine()) != null) {
                sb.append(line);
            }

            // 瑙ｆ瀽鍙傛暟
            java.util.Map map = gson.fromJson(sb.toString(), java.util.Map.class);
            Object commentIdObj = map.get("commentId");

            if (commentIdObj == null) {
                resp.setStatus(400);
                out.print(ResultUtil.fail(400, "璇勮ID涓嶈兘涓虹┖").toJson());
                return;
            }

            Integer commentId = ((Number) commentIdObj).intValue();

            CommentLikeDTO dto = new CommentLikeDTO();
            dto.setCommentId(commentId);
            dto.setUserId(userId);

            boolean success = likeService.unlike(dto);

            if (success) {
                out.print(ResultUtil.success("鍙栨秷鐐硅禐鎴愬姛", null).toJson());
            } else {
                resp.setStatus(500);
                out.print(ResultUtil.fail(500, "鍙栨秷鐐硅禐澶辫触").toJson());
            }

        } catch (BusinessException e) {
            resp.setStatus(e.getCode());
            out.print(ResultUtil.fail(e.getCode(), e.getMessage()).toJson());
        } catch (Exception e) {
            resp.setStatus(500);
            out.print(ResultUtil.fail(500, "绯荤粺閿欒锛? + e.getMessage()).toJson());
        }
    }
}

===== FILE: .\XianYuMarket\src\main\java\com\klei\goodfish\controller\FavoriteCancelServlet.java =====

package com.klei.goodfish.controller;

import com.google.gson.Gson;
import com.klei.goodfish.dto.FavoriteAddDTO;  // 鈽呪槄鈽?鏀圭敤 FavoriteAddDTO
import com.klei.goodfish.service.FavoriteService;
import com.klei.goodfish.service.impl.FavoriteServiceImpl;
import com.klei.goodfish.util.BusinessException;
import com.klei.goodfish.util.ResultUtil;

import javax.servlet.ServletException;
import javax.servlet.annotation.WebServlet;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpServlet;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpServletRequest;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpServletResponse;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpSession;
import java.io.BufferedReader;
import java.io.IOException;
import java.io.PrintWriter;

@WebServlet("/favorite/cancel")
public class FavoriteCancelServlet extends HttpServlet {

    private FavoriteService favoriteService = new FavoriteServiceImpl();
    private Gson gson = new Gson();

    @Override
    protected void doPost(HttpServletRequest req, HttpServletResponse resp)
            throws ServletException, IOException {

        req.setCharacterEncoding("UTF-8");
        resp.setContentType("application/json;charset=UTF-8");
        PrintWriter out = resp.getWriter();

        try {
            HttpSession session = req.getSession();
            Integer userId = (Integer) session.getAttribute("userId");

            if (userId == null) {
                resp.setStatus(401);
                out.print(ResultUtil.fail(401, "璇峰厛鐧诲綍").toJson());
                return;
            }

            // 璇诲彇 JSON
            StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
            BufferedReader reader = req.getReader();
            String line;
            while ((line = reader.readLine()) != null) {
                sb.append(line);
            }

            // 鈽呪槄鈽?浣跨敤 FavoriteAddDTO锛堝寘鍚?userId 鍜?goodId锛?
            FavoriteAddDTO dto = gson.fromJson(sb.toString(), FavoriteAddDTO.class);
            dto.setUserId(userId);  // 浠?Session 娉ㄥ叆褰撳墠鐢ㄦ埛

            if (dto.getGoodId() == null) {
                resp.setStatus(400);
                out.print(ResultUtil.fail(400, "鍟嗗搧ID涓嶈兘涓虹┖").toJson());
                return;
            }

            // 璋冪敤 Service 鐨?cancelFavorite锛堝弬鏁版槸 FavoriteAddDTO锛?
            boolean success = favoriteService.cancelFavorite(dto);

            if (success) {
                out.print(ResultUtil.success("鍙栨秷鏀惰棌鎴愬姛", null).toJson());
            } else {
                resp.setStatus(500);
                out.print(ResultUtil.fail(500, "鍙栨秷鏀惰棌澶辫触").toJson());
            }

        } catch (BusinessException e) {
            resp.setStatus(e.getCode());
            out.print(ResultUtil.fail(e.getCode(), e.getMessage()).toJson());
        } catch (Exception e) {
            resp.setStatus(500);
            out.print(ResultUtil.fail(500, "绯荤粺閿欒锛? + e.getMessage()).toJson());
        }
    }
}



===== FILE: .\XianYuMarket\src\main\java\com\klei\goodfish\controller\FollowAddServlet.java =====

package com.klei.goodfish.controller;

import com.google.gson.Gson;
import com.klei.goodfish.dto.FollowAddDTO;
import com.klei.goodfish.service.FollowService;
import com.klei.goodfish.service.impl.FollowServiceImpl;
import com.klei.goodfish.util.BusinessException;
import com.klei.goodfish.util.ResultUtil;

import javax.servlet.ServletException;
import javax.servlet.annotation.WebServlet;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpServlet;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpServletRequest;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpServletResponse;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpSession;
import java.io.BufferedReader;
import java.io.IOException;
import java.io.PrintWriter;

/**
 * 鍏虫敞鐢ㄦ埛锛堥渶鐧诲綍锛?
 * POST /follow/add
 * Body: {"followingId":2}  // 琚叧娉ㄨ€匢D
 * @author klei
 */
@WebServlet("/follow/add")
public class FollowAddServlet extends HttpServlet {

    private FollowService followService = new FollowServiceImpl();
    private Gson gson = new Gson();

    @Override
    protected void doPost(HttpServletRequest req, HttpServletResponse resp)
            throws ServletException, IOException {

        req.setCharacterEncoding("UTF-8");
        resp.setContentType("application/json;charset=UTF-8");
        PrintWriter out = resp.getWriter();

        try {
            // 浠?Session 鑾峰彇褰撳墠鐧诲綍鐢ㄦ埛ID锛堝叧娉ㄨ€咃級
            HttpSession session = req.getSession(false);
            if (session == null) {
                System.err.println("[FollowAdd] Session is null");
                resp.setStatus(401);
                out.print(ResultUtil.fail(401, "璇峰厛鐧诲綍").toJson());
                return;
            }

            Integer followerId = (Integer) session.getAttribute("userId");
            if (followerId == null) {
                System.err.println("[FollowAdd] userId in session is null");
                resp.setStatus(401);
                out.print(ResultUtil.fail(401, "璇峰厛鐧诲綍").toJson());
                return;
            }

            System.out.println("[FollowAdd] Current userId from session: " + followerId);

            // 璇诲彇 JSON
            StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
            BufferedReader reader = req.getReader();
            String line;
            while ((line = reader.readLine()) != null) {
                sb.append(line);
            }

            String jsonBody = sb.toString();
            System.out.println("[FollowAdd] Request body: " + jsonBody);

            // 杞珼TO
            FollowAddDTO dto = gson.fromJson(jsonBody, FollowAddDTO.class);
            dto.setFollowerId(followerId); // 瀹夊叏澶勭悊锛氫互 Session 涓哄噯

            System.out.println("[FollowAdd] DTO: followerId=" + dto.getFollowerId() + ", followingId=" + dto.getFollowingId());

            // 鍙傛暟鏍￠獙
            if (dto.getFollowingId() == null) {
                resp.setStatus(400);
                out.print(ResultUtil.fail(400, "琚叧娉ㄨ€匢D涓嶈兘涓虹┖").toJson());
                return;
            }

            if (dto.getFollowerId().equals(dto.getFollowingId())) {
                resp.setStatus(400);
                out.print(ResultUtil.fail(400, "涓嶈兘鍏虫敞鑷繁").toJson());
                return;
            }

            // 璋冪敤 Service
            boolean success = followService.follow(dto);

            if (success) {
                out.print(ResultUtil.success("鍏虫敞鎴愬姛", null).toJson());
            } else {
                resp.setStatus(500);
                out.print(ResultUtil.fail(500, "鍏虫敞澶辫触").toJson());
            }

        } catch (BusinessException e) {
            System.err.println("[FollowAdd] BusinessException: " + e.getMessage());
            resp.setStatus(e.getCode());
            out.print(ResultUtil.fail(e.getCode(), e.getMessage()).toJson());
        } catch (Exception e) {
            System.err.println("[FollowAdd] Exception: " + e.getMessage());
            e.printStackTrace();
            resp.setStatus(500);
            out.print(ResultUtil.fail(500, "绯荤粺閿欒: " + e.getMessage()).toJson());
        }
    }

    @Override
    protected void doGet(HttpServletRequest req, HttpServletResponse resp)
            throws ServletException, IOException {
        resp.setContentType("application/json;charset=UTF-8");
        resp.setStatus(405);
        resp.getWriter().print(ResultUtil.fail(405, "璇蜂娇鐢?POST 鏂规硶").toJson());
    }
}



===== FILE: .\XianYuMarket\src\main\java\com\klei\goodfish\controller\FollowCancelServlet.java =====

package com.klei.goodfish.controller;

import com.google.gson.Gson;
import com.klei.goodfish.dto.FollowAddDTO;
import com.klei.goodfish.service.FollowService;
import com.klei.goodfish.service.impl.FollowServiceImpl;
import com.klei.goodfish.util.BusinessException;
import com.klei.goodfish.util.ResultUtil;

import javax.servlet.ServletException;
import javax.servlet.annotation.WebServlet;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpServlet;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpServletRequest;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpServletResponse;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpSession;
import java.io.BufferedReader;
import java.io.IOException;
import java.io.PrintWriter;

@WebServlet("/follow/cancel")
public class FollowCancelServlet extends HttpServlet {

    private FollowService followService = new FollowServiceImpl();
    private Gson gson = new Gson();

    @Override
    protected void doPost(HttpServletRequest req, HttpServletResponse resp)
            throws ServletException, IOException {

        req.setCharacterEncoding("UTF-8");
        resp.setContentType("application/json;charset=UTF-8");
        PrintWriter out = resp.getWriter();

        try {
            HttpSession session = req.getSession();
            Integer userId = (Integer) session.getAttribute("userId");

            if (userId == null) {
                resp.setStatus(401);
                out.print(ResultUtil.fail(401, "璇峰厛鐧诲綍").toJson());
                return;
            }

            // 璇诲彇JSON
            StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
            BufferedReader reader = req.getReader();
            String line;
            while ((line = reader.readLine()) != null) {
                sb.append(line);
            }

            // 鍓嶇浼犵殑鏄?{userId: xxx}锛岄渶瑕佽浆鎴?FollowAddDTO 鐨勫瓧娈靛悕
            // 鎵嬪姩瑙ｆ瀽鎴栦娇鐢∕ap涓浆
            java.util.Map map = gson.fromJson(sb.toString(), java.util.Map.class);

            FollowAddDTO dto = new FollowAddDTO();
            dto.setFollowerId(userId);  // 褰撳墠鐧诲綍鐢ㄦ埛锛堝叧娉ㄨ€咃級

            // 鍓嶇浼犵殑琚叧娉ㄨ€匢D鍙兘鏄?userId 鎴?followingId
            Object targetId = map.get("userId");
            if (targetId == null) {
                targetId = map.get("followingId");
            }

            if (targetId == null) {
                resp.setStatus(400);
                out.print(ResultUtil.fail(400, "鐢ㄦ埛ID涓嶈兘涓虹┖").toJson());
                return;
            }

            dto.setFollowingId(((Number) targetId).intValue());

            // 鈽呪槄鈽?璋冪敤浣犵幇鏈夌殑 unfollow 鏂规硶 鈽呪槄鈽?
            boolean success = followService.unfollow(dto);

            if (success) {
                out.print(ResultUtil.success("鍙栨秷鍏虫敞鎴愬姛", null).toJson());
            } else {
                resp.setStatus(500);
                out.print(ResultUtil.fail(500, "鍙栨秷鍏虫敞澶辫触").toJson());
            }

        } catch (BusinessException e) {
            resp.setStatus(e.getCode());
            out.print(ResultUtil.fail(e.getCode(), e.getMessage()).toJson());
        } catch (Exception e) {
            resp.setStatus(500);
            out.print(ResultUtil.fail(500, "绯荤粺閿欒锛? + e.getMessage()).toJson());
        }
    }

    @Override
    protected void doGet(HttpServletRequest req, HttpServletResponse resp)
            throws ServletException, IOException {
        resp.setContentType("application/json;charset=UTF-8");
        resp.setStatus(405);
        resp.getWriter().print(ResultUtil.fail(405, "璇蜂娇鐢≒OST鏂规硶").toJson());
    }
}

===== FILE: .\XianYuMarket\src\main\java\com\klei\goodfish\controller\GoodDeleteServlet.java =====

package com.klei.goodfish.controller;

import com.google.gson.Gson;
import com.klei.goodfish.service.GoodService;
import com.klei.goodfish.service.impl.GoodServiceImpl;
import com.klei.goodfish.util.BusinessException;
import com.klei.goodfish.util.ResultUtil;

import javax.servlet.ServletException;
import javax.servlet.annotation.WebServlet;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpServlet;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpServletRequest;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpServletResponse;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpSession;
import java.io.BufferedReader;
import java.io.IOException;
import java.io.PrintWriter;

/**
 * 鍒犻櫎鍟嗗搧锛堥€昏緫鍒犻櫎锛岄渶鐧诲綍锛屽彧鑳藉垹闄よ嚜宸辩殑鍟嗗搧鎴栫鐞嗗憳锛?
 * POST /good/delete
 * Body: {"goodId":1}
 * @author klei
 */
@WebServlet("/good/delete")
public class GoodDeleteServlet extends HttpServlet {

    private GoodService goodService = new GoodServiceImpl();
    private Gson gson = new Gson();

    @Override
    protected void doPost(HttpServletRequest req, HttpServletResponse resp)
            throws ServletException, IOException {

        req.setCharacterEncoding("UTF-8");
        resp.setContentType("application/json;charset=UTF-8");
        PrintWriter out = resp.getWriter();

        try {
            // 浠?Session 鑾峰彇褰撳墠鐧诲綍鐢ㄦ埛ID
            HttpSession session = req.getSession();
            Integer userId = (Integer) session.getAttribute("userId");

            // 璇诲彇 JSON 鑾峰彇 goodId
            StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
            BufferedReader reader = req.getReader();
            String line;
            while ((line = reader.readLine()) != null) {
                sb.append(line);
            }

            // 绠€鍗曡В鏋?JSON 鑾峰彇 goodId
            String json = sb.toString();
            Integer goodId = null;
            if (json.contains("\"goodId\"")) {
                String value = json.replaceAll(".*\"goodId\"\\s*:\\s*(\\d+).*", "$1");
                goodId = Integer.parseInt(value);
            }

            if (goodId == null) {
                resp.setStatus(400);
                out.print(ResultUtil.fail(400, "鍟嗗搧ID涓嶈兘涓虹┖").toJson());
                return;
            }

            // 璋冪敤 Service 鍒犻櫎
            boolean success = goodService.deleteGood(goodId, userId);

            if (success) {
                out.print(ResultUtil.success("鍒犻櫎鎴愬姛", null).toJson());
            } else {
                resp.setStatus(500);
                out.print(ResultUtil.fail(500, "鍒犻櫎澶辫触").toJson());
            }

        } catch (BusinessException e) {
            resp.setStatus(e.getCode());
            out.print(ResultUtil.fail(e.getCode(), e.getMessage()).toJson());
        } catch (Exception e) {
            resp.setStatus(500);
            out.print(ResultUtil.fail(500, "鍒犻櫎澶辫触锛? + e.getMessage()).toJson());
        }
    }
}



===== FILE: .\XianYuMarket\src\main\java\com\klei\goodfish\controller\GoodDetailServlet.java =====

package com.klei.goodfish.controller;

import com.google.gson.Gson;
import com.klei.goodfish.entity.Good;
import com.klei.goodfish.entity.User;
import com.klei.goodfish.mapper.UserMapper;
import com.klei.goodfish.mappercore.proxy.MapperProxy;
import com.klei.goodfish.service.FollowService;
import com.klei.goodfish.service.impl.FollowServiceImpl;
import com.klei.goodfish.util.BusinessException;
import com.klei.goodfish.util.ResultUtil;

import javax.servlet.ServletException;
import javax.servlet.annotation.WebServlet;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpServlet;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpServletRequest;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpServletResponse;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpSession;
import java.io.IOException;
import java.io.PrintWriter;
import java.time.format.DateTimeFormatter;
import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.Map;

/**
 * 鏌ヨ鍟嗗搧璇︽儏锛堟棤闇€鐧诲綍锛?
 * GET /good/detail?id=1
 */
@WebServlet("/good/detail")
public class GoodDetailServlet extends HttpServlet {

    private Gson gson = new Gson();
    private static final DateTimeFormatter formatter = DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss");
    private UserMapper userMapper = MapperProxy.getMapper(UserMapper.class);
    private FollowService followService = new FollowServiceImpl();

    @Override
    protected void doGet(HttpServletRequest req, HttpServletResponse resp)
            throws ServletException, IOException {

        req.setCharacterEncoding("UTF-8");
        resp.setContentType("application/json;charset=UTF-8");
        PrintWriter out = resp.getWriter();

        try {
            String idStr = req.getParameter("id");
            if (idStr == null || idStr.trim().isEmpty()) {
                resp.setStatus(400);
                out.print(ResultUtil.fail(400, "鍟嗗搧ID涓嶈兘涓虹┖").toJson());
                return;
            }

            Integer goodId = Integer.parseInt(idStr);

            // 鏌ヨ鍟嗗搧锛堝鐢ㄧ幇鏈夌殑 mapper 鏌ヨ锛岄伩鍏嶅惊鐜緷璧栵級
            // 鐩存帴鐢?mapper 鏌ヨ锛屼笉缁忚繃 service 灞傦紝閬垮厤 service 鐨勬潈闄愭鏌?
            com.klei.goodfish.mapper.GoodMapper goodMapper = MapperProxy.getMapper(com.klei.goodfish.mapper.GoodMapper.class);
            Good good = goodMapper.findById(goodId);

            if (good == null || good.getStatus() == 0) {
                resp.setStatus(404);
                out.print(ResultUtil.fail(404, "鍟嗗搧涓嶅瓨鍦ㄦ垨宸蹭笅鏋?).toJson());
                return;
            }

            // 杞崲涓?Map
            Map<String, Object> result = new HashMap<>();
            result.put("id", good.getId());
            result.put("goodName", good.getGoodName());
            result.put("goodImage", good.getGoodImage());
            result.put("goodPrice", good.getGoodPrice());
            result.put("description", good.getDescription());
            result.put("status", good.getStatus());
            result.put("sellerId", good.getSellerId());
            result.put("sellingStatus", good.getSellingStatus());

            if (good.getCreateTime() != null) {
                result.put("createTime", good.getCreateTime().format(formatter));
            } else {
                result.put("createTime", "");
            }

            // 鏌ヨ鍗栧淇℃伅
            User seller = userMapper.findById(good.getSellerId());
            Map<String, Object> sellerInfo = new HashMap<>();
            if (seller != null) {
                sellerInfo.put("userId", seller.getId());
                sellerInfo.put("userName", seller.getUserName());
                sellerInfo.put("avatar", seller.getAvatar());
            } else {
                sellerInfo.put("userId", good.getSellerId());
                sellerInfo.put("userName", "鏈煡鐢ㄦ埛");
                sellerInfo.put("avatar", "");
            }
            result.put("sellerInfo", sellerInfo);

            // 鈽呪槄鈽?鍏抽敭鏂板锛氬鏋滅敤鎴峰凡鐧诲綍锛屾煡璇㈡槸鍚﹀凡鍏虫敞璇ュ崠瀹?鈽呪槄鈽?
            HttpSession session = req.getSession(false);
            if (session != null) {
                Integer currentUserId = (Integer) session.getAttribute("userId");
                if (currentUserId != null && seller != null) {
                    boolean isFollowing = followService.isFollowing(currentUserId, seller.getId());
                    sellerInfo.put("isFollowing", isFollowing);

                    // 鍚屾椂杩斿洖褰撳墠鐢ㄦ埛ID锛屾柟渚垮墠绔娇鐢?
                    result.put("currentUserId", currentUserId);
                }
            }

            out.print(ResultUtil.success("鏌ヨ鎴愬姛", result).toJson());

        } catch (NumberFormatException e) {
            resp.setStatus(400);
            out.print(ResultUtil.fail(400, "鍟嗗搧ID鏍煎紡閿欒").toJson());
        } catch (BusinessException e) {
            resp.setStatus(e.getCode());
            out.print(ResultUtil.fail(e.getCode(), e.getMessage()).toJson());
        } catch (Exception e) {
            e.printStackTrace();
            resp.setStatus(500);
            out.print(ResultUtil.fail(500, "绯荤粺閿欒锛? + e.getMessage()).toJson());
        }
    }

    @Override
    protected void doPost(HttpServletRequest req, HttpServletResponse resp)
            throws ServletException, IOException {
        resp.setContentType("application/json;charset=UTF-8");
        resp.setStatus(405);
        resp.getWriter().print(ResultUtil.fail(405, "璇蜂娇鐢?GET 鏂规硶").toJson());
    }
}



===== FILE: .\XianYuMarket\src\main\java\com\klei\goodfish\controller\GoodPublishServlet.java =====

package com.klei.goodfish.controller;

import com.google.gson.Gson;
import com.klei.goodfish.dto.GoodPublishDTO;
import com.klei.goodfish.entity.Good;
import com.klei.goodfish.service.GoodService;
import com.klei.goodfish.service.impl.GoodServiceImpl;
import com.klei.goodfish.util.BusinessException;
import com.klei.goodfish.util.ResultUtil;

import javax.servlet.ServletException;
import javax.servlet.annotation.WebServlet;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpServlet;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpServletRequest;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpServletResponse;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpSession;
import java.io.BufferedReader;
import java.io.IOException;
import java.io.PrintWriter;
import java.time.format.DateTimeFormatter;
import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.Map;

/**
 * 鍙戝竷鍟嗗搧锛堥渶鐧诲綍锛?
 */
@WebServlet("/good/publish")
public class GoodPublishServlet extends HttpServlet {

    private GoodService goodService = new GoodServiceImpl();
    private Gson gson = new Gson();
    private static final DateTimeFormatter formatter = DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss");

    @Override
    protected void doPost(HttpServletRequest req, HttpServletResponse resp)
            throws ServletException, IOException {

        req.setCharacterEncoding("UTF-8");
        resp.setContentType("application/json;charset=UTF-8");
        PrintWriter out = resp.getWriter();

        try {
            HttpSession session = req.getSession();
            Integer sellerId = (Integer) session.getAttribute("userId");

            if (sellerId == null) {
                resp.setStatus(401);
                out.print(ResultUtil.fail(401, "璇峰厛鐧诲綍").toJson());
                return;
            }

            StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
            BufferedReader reader = req.getReader();
            String line;
            while ((line = reader.readLine()) != null) {
                sb.append(line);
            }

            GoodPublishDTO dto = gson.fromJson(sb.toString(), GoodPublishDTO.class);
            dto.setSellerId(sellerId);

            Good good = goodService.publishGood(dto);

            // 杞崲涓?Map锛屽鐞?null 鍊?
            Map<String, Object> result = new HashMap<>();
            result.put("id", good.getId());
            result.put("goodName", good.getGoodName());
            result.put("goodImage", good.getGoodImage());
            result.put("goodPrice", good.getGoodPrice());
            result.put("description", good.getDescription());
            result.put("status", good.getStatus());
            result.put("sellerId", good.getSellerId());
            result.put("sellingStatus", good.getSellingStatus());
            // 鍏抽敭淇锛氬垽鏂?null
            if (good.getCreateTime() != null) {
                result.put("createTime", good.getCreateTime().format(formatter));
            } else {
                result.put("createTime", "");
            }

            out.print(ResultUtil.success("鍙戝竷鎴愬姛", result).toJson());

        } catch (BusinessException e) {
            resp.setStatus(e.getCode());
            out.print(ResultUtil.fail(e.getCode(), e.getMessage()).toJson());
        } catch (Exception e) {
            e.printStackTrace();
            resp.setStatus(500);
            out.print(ResultUtil.fail(500, "鍙戝竷澶辫触锛? + e.getMessage()).toJson());
        }
    }

    @Override
    protected void doGet(HttpServletRequest req, HttpServletResponse resp)
            throws ServletException, IOException {
        resp.setContentType("application/json;charset=UTF-8");
        resp.setStatus(405);
        resp.getWriter().print(ResultUtil.fail(405, "璇蜂娇鐢?POST 鏂规硶").toJson());
    }
}

package com.klei.goodfish.controller;

import com.google.gson.Gson;

import javax.servlet.ServletException;
import javax.servlet.annotation.WebServlet;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpServlet;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpServletRequest;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpServletResponse;
import java.io.IOException;
import java.io.PrintWriter;
import java.time.format.DateTimeFormatter;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;

/**
 * 闅忔満鎺ㄨ崘鍟嗗搧锛堟棤闇€鐧诲綍锛?
 */
@WebServlet("/good/random")
public class GoodRandomServlet extends HttpServlet {

    private GoodService goodService = new GoodServiceImpl();
    private Gson gson = new Gson();
    private static final DateTimeFormatter formatter = DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss");

    @Override
    protected void doGet(HttpServletRequest req, HttpServletResponse resp)
            throws ServletException, IOException {

        req.setCharacterEncoding("UTF-8");
        resp.setContentType("application/json;charset=UTF-8");
        PrintWriter out = resp.getWriter();

        try {
            String limitStr = req.getParameter("limit");
            Integer limit = 8;
            if (limitStr != null && !limitStr.trim().isEmpty()) {
                limit = Integer.parseInt(limitStr);
            }

            List<Good> goods = goodService.getRandomGoods(limit);

            // 杞崲涓?List<Map>锛屽鐞?null 鍊?
            List<Map<String, Object>> resultList = new ArrayList<>();
            for (Good good : goods) {
                Map<String, Object> map = new HashMap<>();
                map.put("id", good.getId());
                map.put("goodName", good.getGoodName());
                map.put("goodImage", good.getGoodImage());
                map.put("goodPrice", good.getGoodPrice());
                map.put("description", good.getDescription());
                map.put("status", good.getStatus());
                map.put("sellerId", good.getSellerId());
                map.put("sellingStatus", good.getSellingStatus());
                // 鍏抽敭淇锛氬垽鏂?null
                if (good.getCreateTime() != null) {
                    map.put("createTime", good.getCreateTime().format(formatter));
                } else {
                    map.put("createTime", "");
                }
                resultList.add(map);
            }

            out.print(ResultUtil.success("鏌ヨ鎴愬姛", resultList).toJson());

        } catch (NumberFormatException e) {
            resp.setStatus(400);
            out.print(ResultUtil.fail(400, "鍙傛暟鏍煎紡閿欒").toJson());
        } catch (BusinessException e) {
            resp.setStatus(e.getCode());
            out.print(ResultUtil.fail(e.getCode(), e.getMessage()).toJson());
        } catch (Exception e) {
            e.printStackTrace();
            resp.setStatus(500);
            out.print(ResultUtil.fail(500, "绯荤粺閿欒").toJson());
        }
    }

    @Override
    protected void doPost(HttpServletRequest req, HttpServletResponse resp)
            throws ServletException, IOException {
        resp.setContentType("application/json;charset=UTF-8");
        resp.setStatus(405);
        resp.getWriter().print(ResultUtil.fail(405, "璇蜂娇鐢?GET 鏂规硶").toJson());

package com.klei.goodfish.controller;

import com.google.gson.Gson;
import com.klei.goodfish.dto.GoodSearchDTO;
import com.klei.goodfish.entity.Good;
import com.klei.goodfish.service.GoodService;
import com.klei.goodfish.service.impl.GoodServiceImpl;
import com.klei.goodfish.util.BusinessException;
import com.klei.goodfish.util.ResultUtil;

import javax.servlet.ServletException;
import javax.servlet.annotation.WebServlet;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpServlet;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpServletRequest;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpServletResponse;
import java.io.IOException;
import java.io.PrintWriter;
import java.time.format.DateTimeFormatter;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;

/**
 * 鎼滅储鍟嗗搧锛堟ā绯婃煡璇紝鏃犻渶鐧诲綍锛?
 * GET /good/search?keyword=iPhone
 * @author klei
 */
@WebServlet("/good/search")
public class GoodSearchServlet extends HttpServlet {

    private GoodService goodService = new GoodServiceImpl();
    private Gson gson = new Gson();
    private static final DateTimeFormatter formatter = DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss");

    @Override
    protected void doGet(HttpServletRequest req, HttpServletResponse resp)
            throws ServletException, IOException {

        req.setCharacterEncoding("UTF-8");
        resp.setContentType("application/json;charset=UTF-8");
        PrintWriter out = resp.getWriter();

        try {
            // 鑾峰彇 keyword 鍙傛暟
            String keyword = req.getParameter("keyword");
            if (keyword == null || keyword.trim().isEmpty()) {
                resp.setStatus(400);
                out.print(ResultUtil.fail(400, "鎼滅储鍏抽敭璇嶄笉鑳戒负绌?).toJson());
                return;
            }

            // 缁勮 DTO
            GoodSearchDTO dto = new GoodSearchDTO();
            dto.setKeyword(keyword.trim());

            // 璋冪敤 Service
            List<Good> goods = goodService.searchGoods(dto);

            // 鍏抽敭锛氳浆鎹负 Map 鍒楄〃锛屽鐞?LocalDateTime 鍜?null 鍊?
            List<Map<String, Object>> resultList = new ArrayList<>();
            for (Good good : goods) {
                Map<String, Object> map = new HashMap<>();
                map.put("id", good.getId());
                map.put("goodName", good.getGoodName());
                map.put("goodImage", good.getGoodImage());
                map.put("goodPrice", good.getGoodPrice());
                map.put("description", good.getDescription());
                map.put("status", good.getStatus());
                map.put("sellerId", good.getSellerId());
                map.put("sellingStatus", good.getSellingStatus());
                // 澶勭悊 null 鍊?
                if (good.getCreateTime() != null) {
                    map.put("createTime", good.getCreateTime().format(formatter));
                } else {
                    map.put("createTime", "");
                }
                resultList.add(map);
            }

            out.print(ResultUtil.success("鎼滅储鎴愬姛", resultList).toJson());

        } catch (BusinessException e) {
            resp.setStatus(e.getCode());
            out.print(ResultUtil.fail(e.getCode(), e.getMessage()).toJson());
        } catch (Exception e) {
            e.printStackTrace();
            resp.setStatus(500);
            out.print(ResultUtil.fail(500, "鎼滅储澶辫触锛? + e.getMessage()).toJson());
        }
    }

    @Override
    protected void doPost(HttpServletRequest req, HttpServletResponse resp)
            throws ServletException, IOException {
        resp.setContentType("application/json;charset=UTF-8");
        resp.setStatus(405);
        resp.getWriter().print(ResultUtil.fail(405, "璇蜂娇鐢?GET 鏂规硶").toJson());
    }
}

import com.klei.goodfish.entity.Good;

                    map.put("createTime", "");

                resultList.add(map);
            }

            out.print(ResultUtil.success("鏌ヨ鎴愬姛", resultList).toJson());

        } catch (NumberFormatException e) {
            resp.setStatus(400);

        } catch (BusinessException e) {
            resp.setStatus(e.getCode());

            e.printStackTrace();

        }

===== FILE: .\XianYuMarket\src\main\java\com\klei\goodfish\controller\GoodUpdateServlet.java =====

package com.klei.goodfish.controller;

import com.google.gson.Gson;
import com.klei.goodfish.dto.GoodUpdateDTO;
import com.klei.goodfish.entity.Good;
import com.klei.goodfish.service.GoodService;
import com.klei.goodfish.service.impl.GoodServiceImpl;
import com.klei.goodfish.util.BusinessException;
import com.klei.goodfish.util.ResultUtil;

import javax.servlet.ServletException;
import javax.servlet.annotation.WebServlet;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpServlet;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpServletRequest;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpServletResponse;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpSession;
import java.io.BufferedReader;
import java.io.IOException;
import java.io.PrintWriter;

/**
 * 缂栬緫鍟嗗搧锛堥渶鐧诲綍锛屽彧鑳界紪杈戣嚜宸辩殑鍟嗗搧锛?
 * POST /good/update
 * Body: {"goodId":1,"goodName":"iPhone15 Pro","image":"http://xxx.jpg","price":6999.00,"description":"鍏ㄦ柊"}
 */
@WebServlet("/good/update")
public class GoodUpdateServlet extends HttpServlet {

    private GoodService goodService = new GoodServiceImpl();
    private Gson gson = new Gson();

    @Override
    protected void doPost(HttpServletRequest req, HttpServletResponse resp)
            throws ServletException, IOException {

        req.setCharacterEncoding("UTF-8");
        resp.setContentType("application/json;charset=UTF-8");
        PrintWriter out = resp.getWriter();

        try {
            // 浠?Session 鑾峰彇褰撳墠鐧诲綍鐢ㄦ埛ID
            HttpSession session = req.getSession();
            Integer userId = (Integer) session.getAttribute("userId");

            // 璇诲彇 JSON
            StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
            BufferedReader reader = req.getReader();
            String line;
            while ((line = reader.readLine()) != null) {
                sb.append(line);
            }

            // 杞?DTO
            GoodUpdateDTO dto = gson.fromJson(sb.toString(), GoodUpdateDTO.class);

            // 瀹夊叏澶勭悊锛歶serId 浠?Session 涓哄噯锛堥槻姝吉閫犳潈闄愶級
            dto.setUserId(userId);

            // 璋冪敤 Service
            Good good = goodService.updateGood(dto);

            out.print(ResultUtil.success("缂栬緫鎴愬姛", good).toJson());

        } catch (BusinessException e) {
            resp.setStatus(e.getCode());
            out.print(ResultUtil.fail(e.getCode(), e.getMessage()).toJson());
        } catch (Exception e) {
            resp.setStatus(500);
            out.print(ResultUtil.fail(500, "缂栬緫澶辫触锛? + e.getMessage()).toJson());
        }
    }
}



===== FILE: .\XianYuMarket\src\main\java\com\klei\goodfish\controller\ImageUploadServlet.java =====

package com.klei.goodfish.controller;

import com.klei.goodfish.util.ResultUtil;

import javax.servlet.ServletException;
import javax.servlet.annotation.MultipartConfig;
import javax.servlet.annotation.WebServlet;
import javax.servlet.http.*;
import java.io.File;
import java.io.IOException;
import java.io.PrintWriter;
import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.UUID;

@WebServlet("/upload/image")
@MultipartConfig(
        fileSizeThreshold = 1024 * 1024,
        maxFileSize = 1024 * 1024 * 5,
        maxRequestSize = 1024 * 1024 * 10
)
public class ImageUploadServlet extends HttpServlet {

    private static final String UPLOAD_DIR = "uploads";

    @Override
    protected void doPost(HttpServletRequest req, HttpServletResponse resp)
            throws ServletException, IOException {

        req.setCharacterEncoding("UTF-8");
        resp.setContentType("application/json;charset=UTF-8");
        PrintWriter out = resp.getWriter();

        try {
            HttpSession session = req.getSession(false);
            if (session == null || session.getAttribute("userId") == null) {
                resp.setStatus(401);
                out.print(ResultUtil.fail(401, "璇峰厛鐧诲綍").toJson());
                return;
            }

            Part filePart = req.getPart("file");
            if (filePart == null || filePart.getSize() == 0) {
                resp.setStatus(400);
                out.print(ResultUtil.fail(400, "璇烽€夋嫨鍥剧墖鏂囦欢").toJson());
                return;
            }

            String contentType = filePart.getContentType();
            if (contentType == null || !contentType.startsWith("image/")) {
                resp.setStatus(400);
                out.print(ResultUtil.fail(400, "鍙兘涓婁紶鍥剧墖鏂囦欢").toJson());
                return;
            }

            String appPath = req.getServletContext().getRealPath("");
            String uploadPath = appPath + File.separator + UPLOAD_DIR;
            File uploadDir = new File(uploadPath);
            if (!uploadDir.exists()) {
                uploadDir.mkdirs();
            }

            String extension = contentType.contains("png") ? ".png" : ".jpg";
            String fileName = UUID.randomUUID().toString() + extension;
            String filePath = uploadPath + File.separator + fileName;

            filePart.write(filePath);

            // 鈽呪槄鈽?鍏抽敭淇锛氭瀯寤哄畬鏁碪RL锛屽寘鍚崗璁€佹湇鍔″櫒鍚嶅拰绔彛 鈽呪槄鈽?
            String scheme = req.getScheme(); // http 鎴?https
            String serverName = req.getServerName(); // localhost
            int serverPort = req.getServerPort(); // 8080
            String fullUrl = scheme + "://" + serverName + ":" + serverPort + "/" + UPLOAD_DIR + "/" + fileName;

            Map<String, Object> data = new HashMap<>();
            data.put("url", fullUrl); // 杩斿洖瀹屾暣URL
            data.put("relativePath", "/" + UPLOAD_DIR + "/" + fileName); // 鍚屾椂杩斿洖鐩稿璺緞澶囩敤
            data.put("fileName", fileName);

            out.print(ResultUtil.success("涓婁紶鎴愬姛", data).toJson());

        } catch (Exception e) {
            resp.setStatus(500);
            out.print(ResultUtil.fail(500, "涓婁紶澶辫触锛? + e.getMessage()).toJson());
        }
    }
}



===== FILE: .\XianYuMarket\src\main\java\com\klei\goodfish\controller\OrderCancelServlet.java =====

package com.klei.goodfish.controller;

import com.google.gson.Gson;
import com.klei.goodfish.dto.OrderConfirmDTO;
import com.klei.goodfish.service.OrderService;
import com.klei.goodfish.service.impl.OrderServiceImpl;
import com.klei.goodfish.util.BusinessException;
import com.klei.goodfish.util.ResultUtil;

import javax.servlet.ServletException;
import javax.servlet.annotation.WebServlet;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpServlet;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpServletRequest;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpServletResponse;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpSession;
import java.io.BufferedReader;
import java.io.IOException;
import java.io.PrintWriter;

/**
 * 鍙栨秷璁㈠崟锛堜拱瀹舵垨鍗栧鍙栨秷锛岄€€娆剧粰涔板锛屽晢鍝佸洖搴擄級
 * POST /order/cancel
 * Body: {"orderId":1}
 * 鍙湁寰呯‘璁ょ殑璁㈠崟鑳藉彇娑堬紝鍙栨秷鍚庨€€娆?
 * @author klei
 */
@WebServlet("/order/cancel")
public class OrderCancelServlet extends HttpServlet {

    private OrderService orderService = new OrderServiceImpl();
    private Gson gson = new Gson();

    @Override
    protected void doPost(HttpServletRequest req, HttpServletResponse resp)
            throws ServletException, IOException {

        req.setCharacterEncoding("UTF-8");
        resp.setContentType("application/json;charset=UTF-8");
        PrintWriter out = resp.getWriter();

        try {
            // 浠?Session 鑾峰彇褰撳墠鐧诲綍鐢ㄦ埛ID锛堢敤浜庢潈闄愭牎楠岋細涔板鎴栧崠瀹堕兘鑳藉彇娑堬級
            HttpSession session = req.getSession();
            Integer userId = (Integer) session.getAttribute("userId");

            // 璇诲彇 JSON
            StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
            BufferedReader reader = req.getReader();
            String line;
            while ((line = reader.readLine()) != null) {
                sb.append(line);
            }

            // 杞?DTO锛堝鐢?OrderConfirmDTO锛屽瓧娈典竴鏍凤級
            OrderConfirmDTO dto = gson.fromJson(sb.toString(), OrderConfirmDTO.class);
            dto.setUserId(userId); // 瀹夊叏澶勭悊锛氫互 Session 涓哄噯

            // 璋冪敤 Service锛堝唴閮ㄩ€€娆剧粰涔板锛屾敼鍟嗗搧鐘舵€佷负"鏈嚭鍞?锛屾敼璁㈠崟鐘舵€佷负宸插彇娑堬級
            boolean success = orderService.cancelOrder(dto);

            if (success) {
                out.print(ResultUtil.success("鍙栨秷鎴愬姛锛屽凡閫€娆?, null).toJson());
            } else {
                resp.setStatus(500);
                out.print(ResultUtil.fail(500, "鍙栨秷澶辫触").toJson());
            }

        } catch (BusinessException e) {
            resp.setStatus(e.getCode());
            out.print(ResultUtil.fail(e.getCode(), e.getMessage()).toJson());
        } catch (Exception e) {
            resp.setStatus(500);
            out.print(ResultUtil.fail(500, "鍙栨秷澶辫触锛? + e.getMessage()).toJson());
        }
    }

    @Override
    protected void doGet(HttpServletRequest req, HttpServletResponse resp)
            throws ServletException, IOException {
        resp.setContentType("application/json;charset=UTF-8");
        resp.setStatus(405);
        resp.getWriter().print(ResultUtil.fail(405, "璇蜂娇鐢?POST 鏂规硶").toJson());
    }

===== FILE: .\XianYuMarket\src\main\java\com\klei\goodfish\controller\OrderCreateServlet.java =====

package com.klei.goodfish.controller;

import com.google.gson.Gson;
import com.klei.goodfish.dto.OrderCreateDTO;
import com.klei.goodfish.entity.Order;
import com.klei.goodfish.service.OrderService;
import com.klei.goodfish.service.impl.OrderServiceImpl;
import com.klei.goodfish.util.BusinessException;
import com.klei.goodfish.util.ResultUtil;

import javax.servlet.ServletException;
import javax.servlet.annotation.WebServlet;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpServlet;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpServletRequest;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpServletResponse;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpSession;
import java.io.BufferedReader;
import java.io.IOException;
import java.io.PrintWriter;
import java.time.format.DateTimeFormatter;
import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.Map;

/**
 * 鍒涘缓璁㈠崟锛堣喘涔板晢鍝侊級
 * POST /order/create
 * Body: {"goodId":1}
 */
@WebServlet("/order/create")
public class OrderCreateServlet extends HttpServlet {

    private OrderService orderService = new OrderServiceImpl();
    private Gson gson = new Gson();
    private static final DateTimeFormatter formatter = DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss");

    @Override
    protected void doPost(HttpServletRequest req, HttpServletResponse resp)
            throws ServletException, IOException {

        req.setCharacterEncoding("UTF-8");
        resp.setContentType("application/json;charset=UTF-8");
        PrintWriter out = resp.getWriter();

        try {
            HttpSession session = req.getSession();
            Integer buyerId = (Integer) session.getAttribute("userId");

            StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
            BufferedReader reader = req.getReader();
            String line;
            while ((line = reader.readLine()) != null) {
                sb.append(line);
            }

            OrderCreateDTO dto = gson.fromJson(sb.toString(), OrderCreateDTO.class);
            dto.setBuyerId(buyerId);

            Order order = orderService.createOrder(dto);

            // 鍏抽敭锛氳浆鎹负 Map锛屽鐞?LocalDateTime
            Map<String, Object> result = new HashMap<>();
            result.put("id", order.getId());
            result.put("goodId", order.getGoodId());
            result.put("buyerId", order.getBuyerId());
            result.put("sellerId", order.getSellerId());
            result.put("price", order.getPrice());
            result.put("status", order.getStatus());
            // 鈽呪槄鈽?淇锛氫慨姝ｄ腑鏂囦贡鐮?鈽呪槄鈽?
            // 0寰呯‘璁?1宸插畬鎴?2宸插彇娑?
            result.put("statusName", getStatusName(order.getStatus()));

            if (order.getCreateTime() != null) {
                result.put("createTime", order.getCreateTime().format(formatter));
            } else {
                result.put("createTime", "");
            }

            out.print(ResultUtil.success("涓嬪崟鎴愬姛锛岀瓑寰呭崠瀹剁‘璁?, result).toJson());

        } catch (BusinessException e) {
            resp.setStatus(e.getCode());
            out.print(ResultUtil.fail(e.getCode(), e.getMessage()).toJson());
        } catch (Exception e) {
            e.printStackTrace();
            resp.setStatus(500);
            out.print(ResultUtil.fail(500, "涓嬪崟澶辫触锛? + e.getMessage()).toJson());
        }
    }

    // 鈽呪槄鈽?淇锛氫慨姝ｄ腑鏂囩姸鎬佸悕绉?鈽呪槄鈽?
    private String getStatusName(Integer status) {
        switch (status) {
            case 0: return "寰呯‘璁?;
            case 1: return "宸插畬鎴?;
            case 2: return "宸插彇娑?;
            default: return "鏈煡";
        }
    }

    @Override
    protected void doGet(HttpServletRequest req, HttpServletResponse resp)
            throws ServletException, IOException {
        resp.setContentType("application/json;charset=UTF-8");
        resp.setStatus(405);
        resp.getWriter().print(ResultUtil.fail(405, "璇蜂娇鐢?POST 鏂规硶").toJson());
    }
}



===== FILE: .\XianYuMarket\src\main\java\com\klei\goodfish\controller\OrderPurchasesServlet.java =====

package com.klei.goodfish.controller;

import com.google.gson.Gson;
import com.google.gson.GsonBuilder;
import com.klei.goodfish.service.OrderService;
import com.klei.goodfish.service.impl.OrderServiceImpl;
import com.klei.goodfish.util.BusinessException;
import com.klei.goodfish.util.ResultUtil;
import com.klei.goodfish.vo.OrderVO;

import javax.servlet.ServletException;
import javax.servlet.annotation.WebServlet;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpServlet;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpServletRequest;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpServletResponse;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpSession;
import java.io.IOException;
import java.io.PrintWriter;
import java.time.LocalDateTime;
import java.time.format.DateTimeFormatter;
import java.util.List;

/**
 * 鑾峰彇鎴戣喘涔扮殑璁㈠崟锛堜拱瀹惰瑙掞級
 * GET /order/purchases
 */
@WebServlet("/order/purchases")
public class OrderPurchasesServlet extends HttpServlet {

    private OrderService orderService = new OrderServiceImpl();

    // 閰嶇疆 Gson 姝ｇ‘澶勭悊 LocalDateTime
    private Gson gson = new GsonBuilder()
            .registerTypeAdapter(LocalDateTime.class, new com.google.gson.JsonSerializer<LocalDateTime>() {
                private final DateTimeFormatter formatter = DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss");
                @Override
                public com.google.gson.JsonElement serialize(LocalDateTime src, java.lang.reflect.Type typeOfSrc, com.google.gson.JsonSerializationContext context) {
                    return new com.google.gson.JsonPrimitive(src.format(formatter));
                }
            })
            .create();

    @Override
    protected void doGet(HttpServletRequest req, HttpServletResponse resp)
            throws ServletException, IOException {

        req.setCharacterEncoding("UTF-8");
        resp.setContentType("application/json;charset=UTF-8");
        PrintWriter out = resp.getWriter();

        try {
            // 浠?Session 鑾峰彇褰撳墠鐧诲綍鐢ㄦ埛ID
            HttpSession session = req.getSession();
            Integer buyerId = (Integer) session.getAttribute("userId");

            if (buyerId == null) {
                resp.setStatus(401);
                out.print(ResultUtil.fail(401, "璇峰厛鐧诲綍").toJson());
                return;
            }

            System.out.println("[OrderPurchases] 鏌ヨ璐拱璁㈠崟, buyerId=" + buyerId);

            // 璋冪敤 Service 鏌ヨ璐拱璁㈠崟
            List<OrderVO> orders = orderService.getMyPurchases(buyerId);

            System.out.println("[OrderPurchases] 鏌ヨ缁撴灉: " + (orders != null ? orders.size() : 0) + " 鏉¤褰?);

            out.print(ResultUtil.success("鏌ヨ鎴愬姛", orders).toJson());

        } catch (BusinessException e) {
            resp.setStatus(e.getCode());
            out.print(ResultUtil.fail(e.getCode(), e.getMessage()).toJson());
        } catch (Exception e) {
            e.printStackTrace();
            resp.setStatus(500);
            out.print(ResultUtil.fail(500, "鏌ヨ澶辫触: " + e.getMessage()).toJson());
        }
    }

    @Override
    protected void doPost(HttpServletRequest req, HttpServletResponse resp)
            throws ServletException, IOException {
        resp.setContentType("application/json;charset=UTF-8");
        resp.setStatus(405);
        resp.getWriter().print(ResultUtil.fail(405, "璇蜂娇鐢?GET 鏂规硶").toJson());
    }
}

package com.klei.goodfish.controller;

import com.google.gson.Gson;
import com.klei.goodfish.dto.TagCreateDTO;
import com.klei.goodfish.service.TagService;
import com.klei.goodfish.service.impl.TagServiceImpl;
import com.klei.goodfish.util.BusinessException;
import com.klei.goodfish.util.ResultUtil;
import com.klei.goodfish.vo.TagVO;

import javax.servlet.ServletException;
import javax.servlet.annotation.WebServlet;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpServlet;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpServletRequest;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpServletResponse;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpSession;
import java.io.BufferedReader;
import java.io.IOException;
import java.io.PrintWriter;

/**
 * 鍒涘缓鏍囩锛堢櫥褰曠敤鎴峰潎鍙垱寤猴級
 * POST /tag/create
 * Body: {"tagName":"鏁扮爜浜у搧"}
 */
@WebServlet("/tag/create")
public class TagCreateServlet extends HttpServlet {

    private TagService tagService = new TagServiceImpl();
    private Gson gson = new Gson();

    @Override
    protected void doPost(HttpServletRequest req, HttpServletResponse resp)
            throws ServletException, IOException {

        req.setCharacterEncoding("UTF-8");
        resp.setContentType("application/json;charset=UTF-8");
        PrintWriter out = resp.getWriter();

        try {
            HttpSession session = req.getSession();
            Integer userId = (Integer) session.getAttribute("userId");

            if (userId == null) {
                resp.setStatus(401);
                out.print(ResultUtil.fail(401, "璇峰厛鐧诲綍").toJson());
                return;
            }

            // 璇诲彇JSON
            StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
            BufferedReader reader = req.getReader();
            String line;
            while ((line = reader.readLine()) != null) {
                sb.append(line);
            }

            // 瑙ｆ瀽
            TagCreateDTO dto = gson.fromJson(sb.toString(), TagCreateDTO.class);
            dto.setUserId(userId); // 瀹夊叏澶勭悊锛氫互Session涓哄噯

            if (dto.getTagName() == null || dto.getTagName().trim().isEmpty()) {
                resp.setStatus(400);
                out.print(ResultUtil.fail(400, "鏍囩鍚嶇О涓嶈兘涓虹┖").toJson());
                return;
            }

            // 璋冪敤Service鍒涘缓鏍囩
            TagVO vo = tagService.createTag(dto);

            out.print(ResultUtil.success("鍒涘缓鎴愬姛", vo).toJson());

        } catch (BusinessException e) {
            resp.setStatus(e.getCode());
            out.print(ResultUtil.fail(e.getCode(), e.getMessage()).toJson());
        } catch (Exception e) {
            e.printStackTrace();
            resp.setStatus(500);
            out.print(ResultUtil.fail(500, "鍒涘缓鏍囩澶辫触锛? + e.getMessage()).toJson());
        }
    }

    @Override
    protected void doGet(HttpServletRequest req, HttpServletResponse resp)
            throws ServletException, IOException {
        resp.setContentType("application/json;charset=UTF-8");
        resp.setStatus(405);
        resp.getWriter().print(ResultUtil.fail(405, "璇蜂娇鐢≒OST鏂规硶").toJson());
    }
}

import com.klei.goodfish.service.UserService;
import com.klei.goodfish.service.impl.UserServiceImpl;
import com.klei.goodfish.util.BusinessException;
import com.klei.goodfish.util.ResultUtil;
import com.klei.goodfish.vo.UserFavoriteVO;

import javax.servlet.ServletException;
import javax.servlet.annotation.WebServlet;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpServlet;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpServletRequest;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpServletResponse;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpSession;
import java.io.IOException;
import java.io.PrintWriter;
import java.time.LocalDateTime;
import java.time.format.DateTimeFormatter;
import java.util.ArrayList;

/**
 * 鏌ョ湅鎴戠殑鏀惰棌鍒楄〃锛堥渶鐧诲綍锛?
 * GET /user/favorite
 * @author klei
 */
@WebServlet("/user/favorite")
public class UserFavoriteServlet extends HttpServlet {

    private UserService userService = new UserServiceImpl();

    // 鈽呪槄鈽?鍏抽敭淇锛氶厤缃?Gson 姝ｇ‘澶勭悊 LocalDateTime 鈽呪槄鈽?
    private Gson gson = new GsonBuilder()
            .registerTypeAdapter(LocalDateTime.class, new com.google.gson.JsonSerializer<LocalDateTime>() {
                private final DateTimeFormatter formatter = DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss");
                @Override
                public com.google.gson.JsonElement serialize(LocalDateTime src, java.lang.reflect.Type typeOfSrc, com.google.gson.JsonSerializationContext context) {
                    return new com.google.gson.JsonPrimitive(src.format(formatter));
                }
            })
            .create();

    @Override
    protected void doGet(HttpServletRequest req, HttpServletResponse resp)
            throws ServletException, IOException {

        req.setCharacterEncoding("UTF-8");
        resp.setContentType("application/json;charset=UTF-8");
        PrintWriter out = resp.getWriter();

        try {
            HttpSession session = req.getSession();
            Integer userId = (Integer) session.getAttribute("userId");

            if (userId == null) {
                resp.setStatus(401);
                out.print(ResultUtil.fail(401, "璇峰厛鐧诲綍").toJson());
                return;
            }

            System.out.println("[UserFavorite] 鏌ヨ鐢ㄦ埛鏀惰棌, userId=" + userId);

            UserFavoriteVO vo = userService.getUserFavorite(userId);

            // 鈽呪槄鈽?鍏抽敭淇锛氱‘淇濊繑鍥炵┖鏁扮粍鑰屼笉鏄?null 鈽呪槄鈽?
            if (vo == null) {
                vo = new UserFavoriteVO();
                vo.setUserId(userId);
                vo.setFavorites(new ArrayList<>());
            }

            if (vo.getFavorites() == null) {
                vo.setFavorites(new ArrayList<>());
            }

            System.out.println("[UserFavorite] 杩斿洖鏀惰棌鏁伴噺: " + vo.getFavorites().size());

            // 浣跨敤鑷畾涔?Gson 搴忓垪鍖?
            String jsonResponse = ResultUtil.success("鏌ヨ鎴愬姛", vo).toJson();
            out.print(jsonResponse);

        } catch (BusinessException e) {
            resp.setStatus(e.getCode());
            out.print(ResultUtil.fail(e.getCode(), e.getMessage()).toJson());
        } catch (Exception e) {
            e.printStackTrace();
            resp.setStatus(500);
            out.print(ResultUtil.fail(500, "绯荤粺閿欒: " + e.getMessage()).toJson());
        }
    }

    @Override
    protected void doPost(HttpServletRequest req, HttpServletResponse resp)
            throws ServletException, IOException {
        resp.setContentType("application/json;charset=UTF-8");
        resp.setStatus(405);
        resp.getWriter().print(ResultUtil.fail(405, "璇蜂娇鐢?GET 鏂规硶").toJson());
    }
}
