===== FILE: .\XianYuMarket\src\main\java\com\klei\goodfish\controller\UserLoginServlet.java =====

package com.klei.goodfish.controller;

import com.google.gson.Gson;
import com.klei.goodfish.dto.UserLoginDTO;
import com.klei.goodfish.service.UserService;
import com.klei.goodfish.service.impl.UserServiceImpl;
import com.klei.goodfish.util.BusinessException;
import com.klei.goodfish.util.ResultUtil;
import com.klei.goodfish.vo.UserLoginVO;

import javax.servlet.ServletException;
import javax.servlet.annotation.WebServlet;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpServlet;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpServletRequest;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpServletResponse;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpSession;
import java.io.BufferedReader;
import java.io.IOException;
import java.io.PrintWriter;

/**
 * 鐢ㄦ埛鐧诲綍
 * POST /user/login
 * Body: {"userName":"xxx","password":"xxx"}
 */
@WebServlet("/user/login")
public class UserLoginServlet extends HttpServlet {

    private UserService userService = new UserServiceImpl();
    private Gson gson = new Gson();

    @Override
    protected void doPost(HttpServletRequest req, HttpServletResponse resp)
            throws ServletException, IOException {

        req.setCharacterEncoding("UTF-8");
        resp.setContentType("application/json;charset=UTF-8");
        PrintWriter out = resp.getWriter();

        try {
            // 璇诲彇 JSON 璇锋眰浣?
            StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
            BufferedReader reader = req.getReader();
            String line;
            while ((line = reader.readLine()) != null) {
                sb.append(line);
            }

            // 杞崲涓?DTO
            UserLoginDTO dto = gson.fromJson(sb.toString(), UserLoginDTO.class);

            // 璋冪敤 Service 鐧诲綍
            UserLoginVO vo = userService.login(dto);

            // 澶勭悊缁撴灉
            if (vo.isSuccess()) {
                // 鐧诲綍鎴愬姛锛屽啓鍏?Session
                HttpSession session = req.getSession();
                session.setAttribute("userId", vo.getUserId());
                session.setAttribute("role", vo.getRole());

                out.print(ResultUtil.success(vo.getMessage(), vo).toJson());
            } else {
                // 鐧诲綍澶辫触锛堢敤鎴峰悕鎴栧瘑鐮侀敊璇級
                resp.setStatus(401);
                out.print(ResultUtil.fail(401, vo.getMessage()).toJson());
            }

        } catch (BusinessException e) {
            resp.setStatus(e.getCode());
            out.print(ResultUtil.fail(e.getCode(), e.getMessage()).toJson());
        } catch (Exception e) {
            resp.setStatus(500);
            out.print(ResultUtil.fail(500, "绯荤粺閿欒").toJson());
        }
    }

    @Override
    protected void doGet(HttpServletRequest req, HttpServletResponse resp)
            throws ServletException, IOException {
        resp.setContentType("application/json;charset=UTF-8");
        resp.setStatus(405);
        resp.getWriter().print(ResultUtil.fail(405, "璇蜂娇鐢?POST 鏂规硶").toJson());
    }
}



===== FILE: .\XianYuMarket\src\main\java\com\klei\goodfish\controller\UserProfileServlet.java =====

package com.klei.goodfish.controller;

import com.google.gson.Gson;
import com.klei.goodfish.service.UserService;
import com.klei.goodfish.service.impl.UserServiceImpl;
import com.klei.goodfish.util.BusinessException;
import com.klei.goodfish.util.ResultUtil;
import com.klei.goodfish.vo.UserProfileVO;

import javax.servlet.ServletException;
import javax.servlet.annotation.WebServlet;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpServlet;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpServletRequest;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpServletResponse;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpSession;
import java.io.IOException;
import java.io.PrintWriter;

/**
 * 鏌ョ湅涓汉淇℃伅锛堥渶鐧诲綍锛?
 * GET /user/profile
 * 浠?Session 鍙?userId锛屾棤闇€浼犲弬
 * @author klei
 */
@WebServlet("/user/profile")
public class UserProfileServlet extends HttpServlet {

    private UserService userService = new UserServiceImpl();
    private Gson gson = new Gson();

    @Override
    protected void doGet(HttpServletRequest req, HttpServletResponse resp)
            throws ServletException, IOException {

        req.setCharacterEncoding("UTF-8");
        resp.setContentType("application/json;charset=UTF-8");
        PrintWriter out = resp.getWriter();

        try {
            // 浠?Session 鑾峰彇褰撳墠鐧诲綍鐢ㄦ埛ID锛團ilter宸叉牎楠岃繃锛岃繖閲屼竴瀹氳兘鍙栧埌锛?
            HttpSession session = req.getSession();
            Integer userId = (Integer) session.getAttribute("userId");

            // 璋冪敤 Service 鏌ヨ涓汉淇℃伅
            UserProfileVO vo = userService.getUser(userId);

            if (vo == null) {
                resp.setStatus(404);
                out.print(ResultUtil.fail(404, "鐢ㄦ埛涓嶅瓨鍦?).toJson());
                return;
            }

            // 杩斿洖鎴愬姛
            out.print(ResultUtil.success("鏌ヨ鎴愬姛", vo).toJson());

        } catch (BusinessException e) {
            resp.setStatus(e.getCode());
            out.print(ResultUtil.fail(e.getCode(), e.getMessage()).toJson());
        } catch (Exception e) {
            resp.setStatus(500);
            out.print(ResultUtil.fail(500, "绯荤粺閿欒").toJson());
        }
    }

    @Override
    protected void doPost(HttpServletRequest req, HttpServletResponse resp)
            throws ServletException, IOException {
        resp.setContentType("application/json;charset=UTF-8");
        resp.setStatus(405);
        resp.getWriter().print(ResultUtil.fail(405, "璇蜂娇鐢?GET 鏂规硶").toJson());
    }
}



===== FILE: .\XianYuMarket\src\main\java\com\klei\goodfish\controller\UserRegisterServlet.java =====

package com.klei.goodfish.controller;

import com.google.gson.Gson;
import com.klei.goodfish.dto.UserRegisterDTO;
import com.klei.goodfish.service.UserService;
import com.klei.goodfish.service.impl.UserServiceImpl;
import com.klei.goodfish.util.BusinessException;
import com.klei.goodfish.util.ResultUtil;
import com.klei.goodfish.vo.UserRegisterVO;

import javax.servlet.ServletException;
import javax.servlet.annotation.WebServlet;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpServlet;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpServletRequest;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpServletResponse;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpSession;
import java.io.BufferedReader;
import java.io.IOException;
import java.io.PrintWriter;

/**
 * 鐢ㄦ埛娉ㄥ唽
 * POST /user/register
 * Body: {"userName":"xxx","password":"xxx","confirmPassword":"xxx","phone":"xxx","role":0}
 * @author klei
 */
@WebServlet("/user/register")
public class UserRegisterServlet extends HttpServlet {

    private UserService userService = new UserServiceImpl();
    private Gson gson = new Gson();

    @Override
    protected void doPost(HttpServletRequest req, HttpServletResponse resp)
            throws ServletException, IOException {

        req.setCharacterEncoding("UTF-8");
        resp.setContentType("application/json;charset=UTF-8");
        PrintWriter out = resp.getWriter();

        try {
            // 璇?JSON
            StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
            BufferedReader reader = req.getReader();
            String line;
            while ((line = reader.readLine()) != null) {
                sb.append(line);
            }

            // 杞?DTO
            UserRegisterDTO dto = gson.fromJson(sb.toString(), UserRegisterDTO.class);

            // 璋?Service锛堣繑鍥?UserRegisterVO锛?
            UserRegisterVO vo = userService.register(dto);

            // 鎴愬姛鍒欏啓 Session锛堣嚜鍔ㄧ櫥褰曪級
            if (vo.isSuccess()) {
                HttpSession session = req.getSession();
                session.setAttribute("userId", vo.getUserId());
            }

            // 杩斿洖缁熶竴鏍煎紡
            if (vo.isSuccess()) {
                out.print(ResultUtil.success(vo.getMessage(), vo).toJson());
            } else {
                resp.setStatus(400);
                out.print(ResultUtil.fail(vo.getMessage()).toJson());
            }

        } catch (BusinessException e) {
            resp.setStatus(e.getCode());
            out.print(ResultUtil.fail(e.getCode(), e.getMessage()).toJson());
        } catch (Exception e) {
            resp.setStatus(500);
            out.print(ResultUtil.fail(500, "绯荤粺閿欒").toJson());
        }
    }
}



===== FILE: .\XianYuMarket\src\main\java\com\klei\goodfish\controller\UserUpdateAvatarServlet.java =====

package com.klei.goodfish.controller;

import com.google.gson.Gson;
import com.klei.goodfish.service.UserService;
import com.klei.goodfish.service.impl.UserServiceImpl;
import com.klei.goodfish.util.BusinessException;
import com.klei.goodfish.util.ResultUtil;

import javax.servlet.ServletException;
import javax.servlet.annotation.WebServlet;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpServlet;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpServletRequest;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpServletResponse;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpSession;
import java.io.BufferedReader;
import java.io.IOException;
import java.io.PrintWriter;
import java.util.Map;

/**
 * 鏇存柊鐢ㄦ埛澶村儚
 * POST /user/updateAvatar
 * Body: {"avatar":"http://localhost:8080/uploads/xxx.jpg"}
 * @author klei
 */
@WebServlet("/user/updateAvatar")
public class UserUpdateAvatarServlet extends HttpServlet {

    private UserService userService = new UserServiceImpl();
    private Gson gson = new Gson();

    @Override
    protected void doPost(HttpServletRequest req, HttpServletResponse resp)
            throws ServletException, IOException {

        req.setCharacterEncoding("UTF-8");
        resp.setContentType("application/json;charset=UTF-8");
        PrintWriter out = resp.getWriter();

        try {
            HttpSession session = req.getSession();
            Integer userId = (Integer) session.getAttribute("userId");

            if (userId == null) {
                resp.setStatus(401);
                out.print(ResultUtil.fail(401, "璇峰厛鐧诲綍").toJson());
                return;
            }

            // 璇诲彇 JSON
            StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
            BufferedReader reader = req.getReader();
            String line;
            while ((line = reader.readLine()) != null) {
                sb.append(line);
            }

            // 瑙ｆ瀽 avatar URL
            Map<String, String> map = gson.fromJson(sb.toString(), Map.class);
            String avatarUrl = map.get("avatar");

            if (avatarUrl == null || avatarUrl.trim().isEmpty()) {
                resp.setStatus(400);
                out.print(ResultUtil.fail(400, "澶村儚URL涓嶈兘涓虹┖").toJson());
                return;
            }

            // 璋冪敤 Service 鏇存柊澶村儚
            boolean success = userService.updateAvatar(userId, avatarUrl.trim());

            if (success) {
                out.print(ResultUtil.success("澶村儚鏇存柊鎴愬姛", null).toJson());
            } else {
                resp.setStatus(500);
                out.print(ResultUtil.fail(500, "澶村儚鏇存柊澶辫触").toJson());
            }

        } catch (BusinessException e) {
            resp.setStatus(e.getCode());
            out.print(ResultUtil.fail(e.getCode(), e.getMessage()).toJson());
        } catch (Exception e) {
            e.printStackTrace();
            resp.setStatus(500);
            out.print(ResultUtil.fail(500, "绯荤粺閿欒锛? + e.getMessage()).toJson());
        }
    }

    @Override
    protected void doGet(HttpServletRequest req, HttpServletResponse resp)
            throws ServletException, IOException {
        resp.setContentType("application/json;charset=UTF-8");
        resp.setStatus(405);
        resp.getWriter().print(ResultUtil.fail(405, "璇蜂娇鐢?POST 鏂规硶").toJson());
    }
}



===== FILE: .\XianYuMarket\src\main\java\com\klei\goodfish\controller\WalletBalanceServlet.java =====

package com.klei.goodfish.controller;

import com.google.gson.Gson;
import com.klei.goodfish.service.WalletService;
import com.klei.goodfish.service.impl.WalletServiceImpl;
import com.klei.goodfish.util.BusinessException;
import com.klei.goodfish.util.ResultUtil;
import com.klei.goodfish.vo.WalletBalanceVO;

import javax.servlet.ServletException;
import javax.servlet.annotation.WebServlet;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpServlet;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpServletRequest;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpServletResponse;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpSession;
import java.io.IOException;
import java.io.PrintWriter;
import java.time.format.DateTimeFormatter;
import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.Map;

/**
 * 鏌ヨ閽卞寘浣欓锛堥渶鐧诲綍锛?
 * GET /wallet/balance
 */
@WebServlet("/wallet/balance")
public class WalletBalanceServlet extends HttpServlet {

    private WalletService walletService = new WalletServiceImpl();
    private Gson gson = new Gson();
    private static final DateTimeFormatter formatter = DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss");

    @Override
    protected void doGet(HttpServletRequest req, HttpServletResponse resp)
            throws ServletException, IOException {

        req.setCharacterEncoding("UTF-8");
        resp.setContentType("application/json;charset=UTF-8");
        PrintWriter out = resp.getWriter();

        try {
            HttpSession session = req.getSession();
            Integer userId = (Integer) session.getAttribute("userId");

            WalletBalanceVO vo = walletService.getBalance(userId);

            // 杞崲涓?Map锛岄槻姝?LocalDateTime 搴忓垪鍖栭棶棰?
            Map<String, Object> result = new HashMap<>();
            result.put("userId", vo.getUserId());
            result.put("balance", vo.getBalance());
            result.put("userName", vo.getUserName());

            // 濡傛灉鏈夋洿鏂版椂闂村瓧娈典篃澶勭悊涓€涓嬶紙鍋囪鏈夛級
            // if (vo.getUpdateTime() != null) {
            //     result.put("updateTime", vo.getUpdateTime().format(formatter));
            // }

            out.print(ResultUtil.success("鏌ヨ鎴愬姛", result).toJson());

        } catch (BusinessException e) {
            resp.setStatus(e.getCode());
            out.print(ResultUtil.fail(e.getCode(), e.getMessage()).toJson());
        } catch (Exception e) {
            e.printStackTrace();
            resp.setStatus(500);
            out.print(ResultUtil.fail(500, "鏌ヨ澶辫触锛? + e.getMessage()).toJson());
        }
    }

    @Override
    protected void doPost(HttpServletRequest req, HttpServletResponse resp)
            throws ServletException, IOException {
        resp.setContentType("application/json;charset=UTF-8");
        resp.setStatus(405);
        resp.getWriter().print(ResultUtil.fail(405, "璇蜂娇鐢?GET 鏂规硶").toJson());
    }
}



===== FILE: .\XianYuMarket\src\main\java\com\klei\goodfish\controller\WalletLogsServlet.java =====

package com.klei.goodfish.controller;

import com.google.gson.Gson;
import com.klei.goodfish.service.WalletService;
import com.klei.goodfish.service.impl.WalletServiceImpl;
import com.klei.goodfish.util.BusinessException;
import com.klei.goodfish.util.ResultUtil;
import com.klei.goodfish.vo.WalletLogVO;

import javax.servlet.ServletException;
import javax.servlet.annotation.WebServlet;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpServlet;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpServletRequest;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpServletResponse;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpSession;
import java.io.IOException;
import java.io.PrintWriter;
import java.util.List;

/**
 * 鏌ヨ閽卞寘娴佹按璁板綍锛堥渶鐧诲綍锛?
 * GET /wallet/logs?type=1
 * type 鍙€夛細1鍏呭€?2璐拱 3鏀跺叆锛屼笉浼犳煡鍏ㄩ儴
 * @author klei
 */
@WebServlet("/wallet/logs")
public class WalletLogsServlet extends HttpServlet {

    private WalletService walletService = new WalletServiceImpl();
    private Gson gson = new Gson();

    @Override
    protected void doGet(HttpServletRequest req, HttpServletResponse resp)
            throws ServletException, IOException {

        req.setCharacterEncoding("UTF-8");
        resp.setContentType("application/json;charset=UTF-8");
        PrintWriter out = resp.getWriter();

        try {
            HttpSession session = req.getSession();
            Integer userId = (Integer) session.getAttribute("userId");

            // 鑾峰彇鍙€夊弬鏁?type
            String typeStr = req.getParameter("type");
            List<WalletLogVO> logs;

            if (typeStr != null && !typeStr.trim().isEmpty()) {
                Integer type = Integer.parseInt(typeStr);
                logs = walletService.getWalletLogsByType(userId, type);
            } else {
                logs = walletService.getWalletLogs(userId);
            }

            out.print(ResultUtil.success("鏌ヨ鎴愬姛", logs).toJson());

        } catch (NumberFormatException e) {
            resp.setStatus(400);
            out.print(ResultUtil.fail(400, "type鍙傛暟鏍煎紡閿欒").toJson());
        } catch (BusinessException e) {
            resp.setStatus(e.getCode());
            out.print(ResultUtil.fail(e.getCode(), e.getMessage()).toJson());
        } catch (Exception e) {
            resp.setStatus(500);
            out.print(ResultUtil.fail(500, "鏌ヨ澶辫触锛? + e.getMessage()).toJson());
        }
    }

    @Override
    protected void doPost(HttpServletRequest req, HttpServletResponse resp)
            throws ServletException, IOException {
        resp.setContentType("application/json;charset=UTF-8");
        resp.setStatus(405);
        resp.getWriter().print(ResultUtil.fail(405, "璇蜂娇鐢?GET 鏂规硶").toJson());
    }
}



===== FILE: .\XianYuMarket\src\main\java\com\klei\goodfish\controller\WalletRechargeServlet.java =====

package com.klei.goodfish.controller;

import com.google.gson.Gson;
import com.klei.goodfish.dto.WalletRechargeDTO;
import com.klei.goodfish.service.WalletService;
import com.klei.goodfish.service.impl.WalletServiceImpl;
import com.klei.goodfish.util.BusinessException;
import com.klei.goodfish.util.ResultUtil;

import javax.servlet.ServletException;
import javax.servlet.annotation.WebServlet;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpServlet;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpServletRequest;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpServletResponse;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpSession;
import java.io.BufferedReader;
import java.io.IOException;
import java.io.PrintWriter;

/**
 * 閽卞寘鍏呭€硷紙闇€鐧诲綍锛?
 * POST /wallet/recharge
 * Body: {"amount":100.00, "remark":"鏀粯瀹濆厖鍊?}
 * @author klei
 */
@WebServlet("/wallet/recharge")
public class WalletRechargeServlet extends HttpServlet {

    private WalletService walletService = new WalletServiceImpl();
    private Gson gson = new Gson();

    @Override
    protected void doPost(HttpServletRequest req, HttpServletResponse resp)
            throws ServletException, IOException {

        req.setCharacterEncoding("UTF-8");
        resp.setContentType("application/json;charset=UTF-8");
        PrintWriter out = resp.getWriter();

        try {
            HttpSession session = req.getSession();
            Integer userId = (Integer) session.getAttribute("userId");

            StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
            BufferedReader reader = req.getReader();
            String line;
            while ((line = reader.readLine()) != null) {
                sb.append(line);
            }

            WalletRechargeDTO dto = gson.fromJson(sb.toString(), WalletRechargeDTO.class);
            dto.setUserId(userId); // 瀹夊叏澶勭悊

            boolean success = walletService.recharge(dto);

            if (success) {
                out.print(ResultUtil.success("鍏呭€兼垚鍔?, null).toJson());
            } else {
                resp.setStatus(500);
                out.print(ResultUtil.fail(500, "鍏呭€煎け璐?).toJson());
            }

        } catch (BusinessException e) {
            resp.setStatus(e.getCode());
            out.print(ResultUtil.fail(e.getCode(), e.getMessage()).toJson());
        } catch (Exception e) {
            resp.setStatus(500);
            out.print(ResultUtil.fail(500, "鍏呭€煎け璐ワ細" + e.getMessage()).toJson());
        }
    }
}

===== FILE: .\XianYuMarket\src\main\java\com\klei\goodfish\dto\UserRegisterDTO.java =====

package com.klei.goodfish.dto;

import lombok.Data;

/**
 * @author klei
 */
@Data
public class UserRegisterDTO {
    private String userName;
    private String password;
    private String confirmPassword;
    private Integer role;

    public String validate() {
        if (userName == null || userName.length() < 3) {
            return "鐢ㄦ埛鍚嶈嚦灏?浣?;
        }
        if (password == null || password.length() < 6) {
            return "瀵嗙爜鑷冲皯6浣?;
        }
        if (!password.equals(confirmPassword)) {
            return "涓ゆ瀵嗙爜涓嶄竴鑷?;
        }
        return null;
    }
}

===== FILE: .\XianYuMarket\src\main\java\com\klei\goodfish\filter\CORSFilter.java =====

package com.klei.goodfish.filter;

import javax.servlet.*;
import javax.servlet.annotation.WebFilter;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpServletRequest;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpServletResponse;
import java.io.IOException;

@WebFilter(filterName = "CorsFilter", urlPatterns = "/*")
public class CORSFilter implements Filter {

    @Override
    public void doFilter(ServletRequest req, ServletResponse res, FilterChain chain)
            throws IOException, ServletException {

        HttpServletRequest request = (HttpServletRequest) req;
        HttpServletResponse response = (HttpServletResponse) res;

        // 蹇呴』鎸囧畾鍏蜂綋鍩熷悕锛屼笉鑳芥槸 *锛屽惁鍒?withCredentials 鏃犳晥
        response.setHeader("Access-Control-Allow-Origin", "http://localhost:5173");
        response.setHeader("Access-Control-Allow-Credentials", "true");
        response.setHeader("Access-Control-Allow-Methods", "GET, POST, PUT, DELETE, OPTIONS");
        response.setHeader("Access-Control-Allow-Headers", "Content-Type, X-Requested-With");

        // 棰勬璇锋眰鐩存帴杩斿洖 200
        if ("OPTIONS".equalsIgnoreCase(request.getMethod())) {
            response.setStatus(HttpServletResponse.SC_OK);
            return;
        }

        chain.doFilter(req, res);
    }

    @Override public void init(FilterConfig filterConfig) throws ServletException {}
    @Override public void destroy() {}
}



===== FILE: .\XianYuMarket\src\main\java\com\klei\goodfish\filter\LoginCheckFilter.java =====

package com.klei.goodfish.filter;

import com.klei.goodfish.util.ResultUtil;
import com.google.gson.Gson;

import javax.servlet.*;
import javax.servlet.annotation.WebFilter;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpServletRequest;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpServletResponse;
import javax.servlet.http.HttpSession;
import java.io.IOException;
import java.io.PrintWriter;
import java.util.Arrays;
import java.util.List;

/**
 * 鐧诲綍鏍￠獙杩囨护鍣?- 鎷︽埅闇€瑕佺櫥褰曠殑鎺ュ彛
 * @author klei
 */
@WebFilter("/*")
public class LoginCheckFilter implements Filter {

    private Gson gson = new Gson();

    // 鐧藉悕鍗曪細涓嶉渶瑕佺櫥褰曞氨鑳借闂殑鎺ュ彛锛堥兘涓嶅甫 /api 鍓嶇紑锛屽洜涓?Vite 宸茬粡 rewrite 鎺変簡锛?
    private static final List<String> WHITE_LIST = Arrays.asList(
            "/user/register",
            "/user/login",
            "/good/detail",
            "/good/random",
            "/good/search",
            "/good/list",
            "/tag/list",
            "/comment/list"
    );

    @Override
    public void doFilter(ServletRequest req, ServletResponse res, FilterChain chain)
            throws IOException, ServletException {

        HttpServletRequest request = (HttpServletRequest) req;
        HttpServletResponse response = (HttpServletResponse) res;

        // 鏀捐 CORS 棰勬璇锋眰
        if ("OPTIONS".equalsIgnoreCase(request.getMethod())) {
            chain.doFilter(req, res);
            return;
        }

        // 鑾峰彇璇锋眰璺緞锛堟爣鍑嗘柟寮忥細鍘绘帀 contextPath锛?
        String path = request.getRequestURI();
        String context = request.getContextPath();

        // 鍘绘帀涓婁笅鏂囪矾寰勶紙濡傛灉閰嶇疆浜嗘牴璺緞 /锛宑ontext 涓虹┖锛宲ath 涓嶅彉锛?
        if (path.startsWith(context)) {
            path = path.substring(context.length());
        }

        System.out.println("[LoginCheckFilter] 璺緞: " + path + ", 鏂规硶: " + request.getMethod());

        // 鐧藉悕鍗曠洿鎺ユ斁琛?
        if (isWhiteList(path)) {
            chain.doFilter(req, res);
            return;
        }

        // 妫€鏌?Session
        HttpSession session = request.getSession(false);
        if (session == null || session.getAttribute("userId") == null) {
            System.out.println("[LoginCheckFilter] 鎷︽埅: " + path + " - 鏈櫥褰?);
            response.setContentType("application/json;charset=UTF-8");
            response.setStatus(401);
            PrintWriter out = response.getWriter();
            out.print(ResultUtil.fail(401, "璇峰厛鐧诲綍").toJson());
            return;
        }

        // 宸茬櫥褰曟斁琛?
        System.out.println("[LoginCheckFilter] 鏀捐: " + path + " - 鐢ㄦ埛ID: " + session.getAttribute("userId"));
        chain.doFilter(req, res);
    }

    private boolean isWhiteList(String path) {
        for (String white : WHITE_LIST) {
            if (path.equals(white) || path.startsWith(white + "/")) {
                return true;
            }
        }
        return false;
    }
}

package com.klei.goodfish.mapper;

import com.klei.goodfish.mappercore.Select;

public interface CommentMapper {

    // 鎻掑叆璇勮
    @Insert("INSERT INTO comment(good_id, user_id, content, status, create_time) " +
            "VALUES(?, ?, ?, 1, NOW())")
    void insert(Integer goodId, Integer userId, String content);

    // 鏍规嵁ID鏌ヨ - 浣跨敤鍒悕纭繚鏄犲皠姝ｇ‘
    @Select("SELECT id, good_id as goodId, user_id as userId, content, status, create_time as createTime " +
            "FROM comment WHERE id = ? AND status = 1")
    Comment findById(Integer id);

    // 鏍规嵁鍟嗗搧ID鏌ユ墍鏈夎瘎璁?- 鍏抽敭淇锛氫娇鐢ㄥ埆鍚嶇‘淇濋┘宄版槧灏?
    @Select("SELECT id, good_id as goodId, user_id as userId, content, status, create_time as createTime " +
            "FROM comment WHERE good_id = ? AND status = 1 ORDER BY create_time DESC")
    List<Comment> findByGoodId(Integer goodId);

    // 鍒犻櫎璇勮
    @Update("UPDATE comment SET status = 0 WHERE id = ?")
    void deleteById(Integer id);

    // 缁熻璇勮鏁?
    @Select("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM comment WHERE good_id = ? AND status = 1")
    int countByGoodId(Integer goodId);
}

package com.klei.goodfish.mapper;

import com.klei.goodfish.entity.Good;
import com.klei.goodfish.mappercore.Insert;
import com.klei.goodfish.mappercore.Select;
import com.klei.goodfish.mappercore.Update;
import com.klei.goodfish.mappercore.Delete;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.util.List;

/**
 * @author klei
 */
public interface GoodMapper {

    // 浣跨敤鍒悕鏄犲皠鍒板疄浣撶被瀛楁
    @Select("SELECT id, good_name as goodName, image as goodImage, " +
            "price as goodPrice, description, selling_status as sellingStatus, " +
            "seller_id as sellerId, status, create_time as createTime " +
            "FROM good WHERE id = ?")
    Good findById(Integer id);

    @Select("SELECT id, good_name as goodName, image as goodImage, " +
            "price as goodPrice, description, selling_status as sellingStatus, " +
            "seller_id as sellerId, status, create_time as createTime " +
            "FROM good WHERE seller_id = ? AND status = 1")
    List<Good> findBySellerId(Integer sellerId);

    // 鍙戝竷鍟嗗搧
    @Insert("INSERT INTO good(good_name, image, price, description, selling_status, seller_id, status, create_time) " +
            "VALUES(?, ?, ?, ?, '鏈嚭鍞?, ?, 1, NOW())")
    void insert(String goodName, String image, BigDecimal price, String description, Integer sellerId);

    // 缂栬緫鍟嗗搧
    @Update("UPDATE good SET good_name = ?, image = ?, price = ?, description = ? WHERE id = ?")
    void update(String goodName, String image, BigDecimal price, String description, Integer id);

    // 鍒犻櫎鍟嗗搧锛堥€昏緫鍒犻櫎锛?
    @Update("UPDATE good SET status = 0 WHERE id = ?")
    void deleteById(Integer id);

    // 鎼滅储鍟嗗搧锛堟ā绯婃煡璇級
    @Select("SELECT id, good_name as goodName, image as goodImage, " +
            "price as goodPrice, description, selling_status as sellingStatus, " +
            "seller_id as sellerId, status, create_time as createTime " +
            "FROM good WHERE good_name LIKE ? AND status = 1")
    List<Good> searchByName(String keyword);

    // 闅忔満鎺ㄨ崘锛堜慨澶嶏細浣跨敤"鏈嚭鍞? 鏇挎崲涔辩爜锛?
    @Select("SELECT id, good_name as goodName, image as goodImage, " +
            "price as goodPrice, description, selling_status as sellingStatus, " +
            "seller_id as sellerId, status, create_time as createTime " +
            "FROM good WHERE selling_status = '鏈嚭鍞? AND status = 1 ORDER BY RAND() LIMIT ?")
    List<Good> findRandom(Integer limit);

    // 鏍规嵁鏍囩ID鏌ュ晢鍝?
    @Select("SELECT g.id, g.good_name as goodName, g.image as goodImage, " +
            "g.price as goodPrice, g.description, g.selling_status as sellingStatus, " +
            "g.seller_id as sellerId, g.status, g.create_time as createTime " +
            "FROM good g JOIN good_tag gt ON g.id = gt.good_id WHERE gt.tag_id = ? AND g.status = 1")
    List<Good> findByTagId(Integer tagId);

    // 鏇存柊閿€鍞姸鎬侊紙淇锛氫娇鐢?鏈嚭鍞?/"宸插嚭鍞? 鏇挎崲涔辩爜锛?
    @Update("UPDATE good SET selling_status = ? WHERE id = ?")
    void updateSellingStatus(String sellingStatus, Integer id);

    // 鈽呪槄鈽?鍏抽敭鏂板锛氬師瀛愭€ф洿鏂板晢鍝佷负宸插嚭鍞紙闃叉骞跺彂閲嶅璐拱锛夆槄鈽呪槄
    // 鍙湁鍦?selling_status = '鏈嚭鍞? 鏃舵墠鏇存柊锛岃繑鍥炲奖鍝嶈鏁帮紙1=鎴愬姛锛?=宸茶涔拌蛋锛?
    @Update("UPDATE good SET selling_status = '宸插嚭鍞? WHERE id = ? AND selling_status = '鏈嚭鍞? AND status = 1")
    int markAsSold(Integer goodId);

    // 鈽呪槄鈽?鍏抽敭鏂板锛氬皢鍟嗗搧鐘舵€佹仮澶嶄负鏈嚭鍞紙鐢ㄤ簬鎵ｆ澶辫触鏃跺洖婊氾級鈽呪槄鈽?
    @Update("UPDATE good SET selling_status = '鏈嚭鍞? WHERE id = ? AND selling_status = '宸插嚭鍞?")
    int markAsAvailable(Integer goodId);
}

    @Select("SELECT * FROM `order` WHERE seller_id = ? ORDER BY create_time DESC")

    @Select("SELECT * FROM `order` WHERE good_id = ? AND status != 2 ORDER BY create_time DESC, id DESC LIMIT 1")
    Order findByGoodId(Integer goodId);

===== FILE: .\XianYuMarket\src\main\java\com\klei\goodfish\mappercore\proxy\MapperProxy.java =====

package com.klei.goodfish.mappercore.proxy;

import com.klei.goodfish.mappercore.*;
import com.klei.goodfish.util.DBUtil;
import java.lang.reflect.*;
import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.sql.Date;
import java.sql.Timestamp;
import java.sql.*;
import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.time.LocalDateTime;
import java.util.*;

public class MapperProxy implements InvocationHandler {

    //璁㏒ervice鑳借幏鍙栦唬鐞?
    public static <T> T getMapper(Class<T> clazz) {
        return (T) Proxy.newProxyInstance(
                clazz.getClassLoader(),
                new Class<?>[]{clazz},
                new MapperProxy()
        );
    }

    @Override
    public Object invoke(Object proxy, Method method, Object[] args) throws Throwable {
        // 鑾峰彇鏂规硶涓婄殑鍚勪釜娉ㄨВ
        Select select = method.getAnnotation(Select.class);
        Insert insert = method.getAnnotation(Insert.class);
        Update update = method.getAnnotation(Update.class);
        Delete delete = method.getAnnotation(Delete.class);

        // 鑾峰彇 SQL 鍜屽垽鏂搷浣滅被鍨?
        String sql = null;

        int operationType = 0;
        if (select != null) {
            sql = select.value();
            operationType = 1;
        } else if (insert != null) {
            sql = insert.value();
            operationType = 2;
        } else if (update != null) {
            sql = update.value();
            operationType = 3;
        } else if (delete != null) {
            sql = delete.value();
            operationType = 4;
        } else {
            return method.invoke(this, args);
        }

        // 鎵ц JDBC
        Connection conn = null;
        PreparedStatement pstmt = null;
        ResultSet rs = null;

        try {
            conn = DBUtil.getConnection();
            pstmt = conn.prepareStatement(sql);

            if (args != null && args.length > 0) {
                for (int i = 0; i < args.length; i++) {
                    pstmt.setObject(i + 1, args[i]);
                }
            }

            // 鏍规嵁鎿嶄綔绫诲瀷鎵ц涓嶅悓閫昏緫
            if (operationType == 1) {
                //杩斿洖缁撴灉闆?
                rs = pstmt.executeQuery();
                return handleResultSet(rs, method);
            } else {
                return pstmt.executeUpdate();
            }

        } finally {
            // 缁熶竴鍏抽棴璧勬簮锛圫elect 鏈?ResultSet锛屽叾瀹冩病鏈夛級
            if (operationType == 1) {
                DBUtil.close(rs, pstmt, conn);
            } else {
                DBUtil.close(null, pstmt, conn);
            }
        }
    }

    // 澶勭悊鏌ヨ缁撴灉 - 鍏抽敭淇锛氬鍔犳爣閲忕被鍨嬪鐞?
    private Object handleResultSet(ResultSet rs, Method method) throws Exception {
        Class<?> returnType = method.getReturnType();

        // ===== 鍏抽敭鏂板锛氬鐞嗚繑鍥炲€间负鏍囬噺绫诲瀷鐨勬儏鍐碉紙濡?int, long, String 绛夛級=====
        if (isScalarType(returnType)) {
            if (rs.next()) {
                Object value = rs.getObject(1);
                return convertToScalar(value, returnType);
            }
            // 濡傛灉娌℃湁缁撴灉锛岃繑鍥為粯璁ゅ€?
            return getDefaultScalarValue(returnType);
        }

        // 鍘熸湁閫昏緫锛氬鐞嗚繑鍥?List<Map> 鎴?鍗曚釜瀵硅薄/Map 鐨勬儏鍐?
        List<Map<String, Object>> list = new ArrayList<>();
        ResultSetMetaData metaData = rs.getMetaData();
        int columnCount = metaData.getColumnCount();

        while (rs.next()) {
            Map<String, Object> row = new HashMap<>();
            for (int i = 1; i <= columnCount; i++) {
                // 鍏抽敭淇敼锛氫娇鐢?getColumnLabel 鑾峰彇 SQL 鍒悕锛坅s goodPrice锛夛紝鑰岄潪鍘熷鍒楀悕锛坧rice锛?
                row.put(metaData.getColumnLabel(i), rs.getObject(i));
            }
            list.add(row);
        }

        // 澶勭悊杩斿洖 List<瀹炰綋> 鐨勬儏鍐碉紙濡傛煡璇㈡敹钘忓垪琛級
        if (returnType == List.class) {
            // 鑾峰彇 List 閲岀殑娉涘瀷绫诲瀷锛堝 List<Good> 涓殑 Good锛?
            Type genericType = method.getGenericReturnType();
            Class<?> actualType = (Class<?>) ((ParameterizedType) genericType).getActualTypeArguments()[0];

            // 濡傛灉娉涘瀷涔熸槸鏍囬噺绫诲瀷锛堝 List<Integer>锛夛紝鐩存帴杩斿洖鍘熷鍊煎垪琛?
            if (isScalarType(actualType)) {
                List<Object> scalarList = new ArrayList<>();
                for (Map<String, Object> row : list) {
                    Object value = row.values().iterator().next(); // 鑾峰彇绗竴鍒楃殑鍊?
                    scalarList.add(convertToScalar(value, actualType));
                }
                return scalarList;
            }

            // 灏?List<Map> 杞垚 List<瀹炰綋>
            List<Object> result = new ArrayList<>();
            for (Map<String, Object> row : list) {
                Object obj = mapToObj(row, actualType);
                result.add(obj);
            }
            return result;
        }

        // 澶勭悊杩斿洖鍗曚釜瀵硅薄鐨勬儏鍐碉紙濡傛煡璇釜浜轰俊鎭級
        if (list.isEmpty()) {
            return null;
        }

        // Java 8 鍏煎鎬э紝get(0) 鏇夸唬 getFirst()
        Map<String, Object> row = list.get(0);
        if (returnType == Map.class) {
            return row;
        }

        return mapToObj(row, returnType);
    }

    // ===== 鏂板锛氬垽鏂槸鍚︿负鏍囬噺绫诲瀷 =====
    private boolean isScalarType(Class<?> clazz) {
        if (clazz == null) {
            return false;
        }
        return clazz.isPrimitive() ||
                clazz == String.class ||
                clazz == Integer.class ||
                clazz == Long.class ||
                clazz == Short.class ||
                clazz == Byte.class ||
                clazz == Float.class ||
                clazz == Double.class ||
                clazz == Boolean.class ||
                clazz == Character.class ||
                clazz == BigDecimal.class ||
                clazz == Date.class ||
                clazz == java.util.Date.class ||
                clazz == Timestamp.class ||
                clazz == LocalDate.class ||
                clazz == LocalDateTime.class;
    }

    // ===== 鏂板锛氬皢鍊艰浆鎹负鏍囬噺绫诲瀷 =====
    private Object convertToScalar(Object value, Class<?> targetType) {
        if (value == null) {
            return getDefaultScalarValue(targetType);
        }

        // 濡傛灉绫诲瀷宸茬粡鍖归厤锛岀洿鎺ヨ繑鍥?
        if (targetType.isAssignableFrom(value.getClass())) {
            return value;
        }

        // 澶勭悊鍩烘湰绫诲瀷鍜屽寘瑁呯被鐨勮浆鎹?
        if (targetType == int.class || targetType == Integer.class) {
            if (value instanceof Number) {
                return ((Number) value).intValue();
            }
            if (value instanceof String) {
                return Integer.parseInt((String) value);
            }
            return Integer.valueOf(value.toString());
        }
        if (targetType == long.class || targetType == Long.class) {
            if (value instanceof Number) {
                return ((Number) value).longValue();
            }
            if (value instanceof String) {
                return Long.parseLong((String) value);
            }
            return Long.valueOf(value.toString());
        }
        if (targetType == short.class || targetType == Short.class) {
            if (value instanceof Number) {
                return ((Number) value).shortValue();
            }
            return Short.valueOf(value.toString());
        }
        if (targetType == byte.class || targetType == Byte.class) {
            if (value instanceof Number) {
                return ((Number) value).byteValue();
            }
            return Byte.valueOf(value.toString());
        }
        if (targetType == float.class || targetType == Float.class) {
            if (value instanceof Number) {
                return ((Number) value).floatValue();
            }
            return Float.valueOf(value.toString());
        }
        if (targetType == double.class || targetType == Double.class) {
            if (value instanceof Number) {
                return ((Number) value).doubleValue();
            }
            return Double.valueOf(value.toString());
        }
        if (targetType == boolean.class || targetType == Boolean.class) {
            if (value instanceof Boolean) {
                return value;
            }
            return Boolean.valueOf(value.toString());
        }
        if (targetType == String.class) {
            return value.toString();
        }

        return value;
    }

    // ===== 鏂板锛氳幏鍙栨爣閲忕被鍨嬬殑榛樿鍊?=====
    private Object getDefaultScalarValue(Class<?> targetType) {
        if (targetType == int.class) {
            return 0;
        }
        if (targetType == long.class) {
            return 0L;
        }
        if (targetType == short.class) {
            return (short) 0;
        }
        if (targetType == byte.class) {
            return (byte) 0;
        }
        if (targetType == float.class) {
            return 0.0f;
        }
        if (targetType == double.class) {
            return 0.0d;
        }
        if (targetType == boolean.class) {
            return false;
        }
        if (targetType == char.class) {
            return '\u0000';
        }
        // 鍖呰绫诲拰 String 杩斿洖 null
        return null;
    }

    // 鎻愬彇鐨勫叕鍏辨柟娉曪細Map 杞璞★紙淇瀛楁鏄犲皠鍜岀被鍨嬭浆鎹級
    private Object mapToObj(Map<String, Object> row, Class<?> clazz) throws Exception {
        Object obj = clazz.getDeclaredConstructor().newInstance();

        for (Map.Entry<String, Object> entry : row.entrySet()) {
            String key = entry.getKey();
            Object value = entry.getValue();

            if (value == null) {
                continue;
            } // 璺宠繃null鍊?

            // 绛栫暐1锛氬厛灏濊瘯鍘熷key锛堝鐞?SQL as 鍒悕锛屽 goodPrice锛?
            Field field = null;
            try {
                field = clazz.getDeclaredField(key);
            } catch (NoSuchFieldException e) {
                // 绛栫暐2锛氬皾璇曢┘宄拌浆鎹紙澶勭悊涓嬪垝绾匡紝濡?good_price -> goodPrice锛?
                String camelKey = toCamelCase(key);
                try {
                    field = clazz.getDeclaredField(camelKey);
                } catch (NoSuchFieldException e2) {
                    // 绛栫暐3锛氬皾璇曞叏灏忓啓鍖归厤
                    try {
                        field = clazz.getDeclaredField(key.toLowerCase());
                    } catch (NoSuchFieldException e3) {
                        // 瀛楁涓嶅瓨鍦紝璺宠繃
                        continue;
                    }
                }
            }

            if (field != null) {
                field.setAccessible(true);

                // 鍏抽敭淇锛氬鐞嗙被鍨嬭浆鎹紙鐗瑰埆鏄疭QL鏃堕棿绫诲瀷鍒癑ava8鏃堕棿API锛?
                Object convertedValue = convertValueToFieldType(value, field.getType());
                if (convertedValue != null) {
                    try {
                        field.set(obj, convertedValue);
                    } catch (IllegalArgumentException e) {
                        // 濡傛灉杞崲鍚庝粛鐒舵棤娉曡缃紝鎵撳嵃鏃ュ織浣嗕笉涓柇锛堥伩鍏?00閿欒锛?
                        System.err.println("瀛楁绫诲瀷涓嶅尮閰? " + field.getName() +
                                ", 鏈熸湜绫诲瀷: " + field.getType().getName() +
                                ", 瀹為檯绫诲瀷: " + value.getClass().getName());
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        return obj;
    }

    // 鍏抽敭淇锛氱被鍨嬭浆鎹㈡柟娉?
    private Object convertValueToFieldType(Object value, Class<?> targetType) {
        if (value == null) {
            return null;
        }

        // 濡傛灉绫诲瀷宸茬粡鍖归厤锛岀洿鎺ヨ繑鍥?
        if (targetType.isAssignableFrom(value.getClass())) {
            return value;
        }

        // 澶勭悊 LocalDateTime锛堟暟鎹簱杩斿洖閫氬父鏄?Timestamp锛?
        if (targetType == LocalDateTime.class) {
            if (value instanceof Timestamp) {
                return ((Timestamp) value).toLocalDateTime();
            }
            if (value instanceof java.sql.Date) {
                return ((java.sql.Date) value).toLocalDate().atStartOfDay();
            }
        }

        // 澶勭悊 LocalDate
        if (targetType == LocalDate.class) {
            if (value instanceof Date) {
                return ((Date) value).toLocalDate();
            }
            if (value instanceof Timestamp) {
                return ((Timestamp) value).toLocalDateTime().toLocalDate();
            }
        }

        // 澶勭悊 BigDecimal 鍒板熀鏈暟瀛楃被鍨嬬殑杞崲
        if (value instanceof BigDecimal) {
            BigDecimal bd = (BigDecimal) value;
            if (targetType == int.class || targetType == Integer.class) {
                return bd.intValue();
            }
            if (targetType == long.class || targetType == Long.class) {
                return bd.longValue();
            }
            if (targetType == double.class || targetType == Double.class) {
                return bd.doubleValue();
            }
            if (targetType == float.class || targetType == Float.class) {
                return bd.floatValue();
            }
        }

        // 澶勭悊 Integer 鍒?Long 绛夛紙鑷姩瑁呯鎷嗙鐨勬墿灞曪級
        if (value instanceof Integer) {
            Integer i = (Integer) value;
            if (targetType == Long.class || targetType == long.class) {
                return i.longValue();
            }
        }

        // 濡傛灉鏃犳硶杞崲锛岃繑鍥炲師鍊硷紙璁?field.set 鑷繁鍐冲畾鏄惁鎶涘嚭寮傚父锛?
        return value;
    }

    // 娣诲姞椹煎嘲鏄犲皠锛堜笅鍒掔嚎杞┘宄帮級
    private String toCamelCase(String str) {
        if (str == null || str.isEmpty()) {
            return str;
        }

        StringBuilder result = new StringBuilder();
        boolean nextUpper = false;

        for (int i = 0; i < str.length(); i++) {
            char c = str.charAt(i);
            if (c == '_') {
                nextUpper = true;
            } else if (nextUpper) {
                result.append(Character.toUpperCase(c));
                nextUpper = false;
            } else {
                result.append(c);
            }
        }

        return result.toString();
    }
}

===== FILE: .\XianYuMarket\src\main\java\com\klei\goodfish\service\UserService.java =====

package com.klei.goodfish.service;

import com.klei.goodfish.dto.UserFavoriteDTO;
import com.klei.goodfish.dto.UserLoginDTO;
import com.klei.goodfish.dto.UserRegisterDTO;
import com.klei.goodfish.vo.*;

/**
 * @author klei

===== FILE: .\XianYuMarket\src\main\java\com\klei\goodfish\service\impl\CommentLikeServiceImpl.java =====

package com.klei.goodfish.service.impl;

import com.klei.goodfish.dto.CommentLikeDTO;
import com.klei.goodfish.entity.Comment;
import com.klei.goodfish.entity.CommentLike;
import com.klei.goodfish.entity.User;
import com.klei.goodfish.mapper.CommentLikeMapper;
import com.klei.goodfish.mapper.CommentMapper;
import com.klei.goodfish.mapper.UserMapper;
import com.klei.goodfish.mappercore.proxy.MapperProxy;
import com.klei.goodfish.service.CommentLikeService;
import com.klei.goodfish.util.BusinessException;
import com.klei.goodfish.vo.LikeStatusVO;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

public class CommentLikeServiceImpl implements CommentLikeService {

    private CommentLikeMapper likeMapper = MapperProxy.getMapper(CommentLikeMapper.class);
    private CommentMapper commentMapper = MapperProxy.getMapper(CommentMapper.class);
    private UserMapper userMapper = MapperProxy.getMapper(UserMapper.class);

    @Override
    public boolean like(CommentLikeDTO dto) {
        if (dto.getCommentId() == null || dto.getUserId() == null) {
            throw new BusinessException(400, "鍙傛暟涓嶈兘涓虹┖");
        }

        // 鏍￠獙璇勮鏄惁瀛樺湪涓旀湭鍒犻櫎
        Comment comment = commentMapper.findById(dto.getCommentId());
        if (comment == null || comment.getStatus() == 0) {
            throw new BusinessException(404, "璇勮涓嶅瓨鍦ㄦ垨宸插垹闄?);
        }

        // 鏍￠獙鐢ㄦ埛鏄惁瀛樺湪
        User user = userMapper.findById(dto.getUserId());
        if (user == null) {
            throw new BusinessException(404, "鐢ㄦ埛涓嶅瓨鍦?);
        }

        // 鏌ラ噸锛氭槸鍚﹀凡鐐硅禐
        CommentLike exist = likeMapper.findByBothId(dto.getCommentId(), dto.getUserId());
        if (exist != null) {
            throw new BusinessException(400, "宸茬偣璧烇紝璇峰嬁閲嶅鐐硅禐");
        }

        // 鎻掑叆鐐硅禐璁板綍
        likeMapper.insert(dto.getCommentId(), dto.getUserId());
        return true;
    }

    @Override
    public boolean unlike(CommentLikeDTO dto) {
        if (dto.getCommentId() == null || dto.getUserId() == null) {
            throw new BusinessException(400, "鍙傛暟涓嶈兘涓虹┖");
        }

        // 鏌ラ噸锛氭槸鍚﹀凡鐐硅禐锛堟病鐐硅禐杩囦笉鑳藉彇娑堬級
        CommentLike exist = likeMapper.findByBothId(dto.getCommentId(), dto.getUserId());
        if (exist == null) {
            throw new BusinessException(400, "鏈偣璧烇紝鏃犳硶鍙栨秷");
        }

        // 鍒犻櫎璁板綍
        likeMapper.delete(dto.getCommentId(), dto.getUserId());
        return true;
    }

    @Override
    public LikeStatusVO getLikeStatus(Integer commentId, Integer userId) {
        if (commentId == null) {
            throw new BusinessException(400, "璇勮ID涓嶈兘涓虹┖");
        }

        LikeStatusVO vo = new LikeStatusVO();

        // 鏌ヨ鎬荤偣璧炴暟
        int count = likeMapper.countByCommentId(commentId);
        vo.setLikeCount(count);

        // 鏌ヨ褰撳墠鐢ㄦ埛鏄惁宸茬偣璧烇紙userId涓簄ull鏃堕粯璁ゆ湭鐐硅禐锛?
        if (userId != null) {
            CommentLike like = likeMapper.findByBothId(commentId, userId);
            vo.setHasLiked(like != null);
        } else {
            vo.setHasLiked(false);
        }

        return vo;
    }

    @Override
    public int getLikeCount(Integer commentId) {
        if (commentId == null) {
            return 0;
        }
        return likeMapper.countByCommentId(commentId);
    }

    @Override
    public List<Integer> getLikedCommentIds(Integer userId) {
        if (userId == null) {
            return new ArrayList<>();
        }
        List<CommentLike> likes = likeMapper.findByUserId(userId);
        List<Integer> commentIds = new ArrayList<>();
        for (CommentLike like : likes) {
            commentIds.add(like.getCommentId());
        }
        return commentIds;
    }
}



===== FILE: .\XianYuMarket\src\main\java\com\klei\goodfish\service\impl\CommentServiceImpl.java =====

package com.klei.goodfish.service.impl;

import com.klei.goodfish.dto.CommentPublishDTO;
import com.klei.goodfish.entity.Comment;
import com.klei.goodfish.entity.Good;
import com.klei.goodfish.entity.User;
import com.klei.goodfish.mapper.CommentMapper;
import com.klei.goodfish.mapper.CommentLikeMapper;
import com.klei.goodfish.mapper.GoodMapper;
import com.klei.goodfish.mapper.UserMapper;
import com.klei.goodfish.mappercore.proxy.MapperProxy;
import com.klei.goodfish.service.CommentService;
import com.klei.goodfish.util.BusinessException;
import com.klei.goodfish.vo.CommentVO;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Objects;

public class CommentServiceImpl implements CommentService {

    private CommentMapper commentMapper = MapperProxy.getMapper(CommentMapper.class);
    private UserMapper userMapper = MapperProxy.getMapper(UserMapper.class);
    private CommentLikeMapper likeMapper = MapperProxy.getMapper(CommentLikeMapper.class);
    private GoodMapper goodMapper = MapperProxy.getMapper(GoodMapper.class);

    @Override
    public CommentVO publishComment(CommentPublishDTO dto) {
        // 鍙傛暟鏍￠獙
        if (dto.getGoodId() == null) {
            throw new BusinessException(400, "鍟嗗搧ID涓嶈兘涓虹┖");
        }
        if (dto.getContent() == null || dto.getContent().trim().isEmpty()) {
            throw new BusinessException(400, "璇勮鍐呭涓嶈兘涓虹┖");
        }
        if (dto.getUserId() == null) {
            throw new BusinessException(400, "鐢ㄦ埛ID涓嶈兘涓虹┖");
        }

        // 鏍￠獙鍟嗗搧鏄惁瀛樺湪涓旀湭涓嬫灦
        Good good = goodMapper.findById(dto.getGoodId());
        if (good == null || good.getStatus() == 0) {
            throw new BusinessException(404, "鍟嗗搧涓嶅瓨鍦ㄦ垨宸蹭笅鏋?);
        }

        // 鏍￠獙鐢ㄦ埛鏄惁瀛樺湪
        User user = userMapper.findById(dto.getUserId());
        if (user == null) {
            throw new BusinessException(404, "鐢ㄦ埛涓嶅瓨鍦?);
        }

        try {
            // 鎻掑叆璇勮
            System.out.println("[CommentService] 鍑嗗鎻掑叆璇勮锛歡oodId=" + dto.getGoodId() + ", userId=" + dto.getUserId());
            commentMapper.insert(dto.getGoodId(), dto.getUserId(), dto.getContent().trim());
            System.out.println("[CommentService] 璇勮鎻掑叆鎴愬姛");

            // 鏌ヨ鍒氭彃鍏ョ殑璇勮锛堥€氳繃鍊掑簭鏌ユ壘褰撳墠鐢ㄦ埛鐨勬渶鏂拌瘎璁猴級
            List<Comment> comments = commentMapper.findByGoodId(dto.getGoodId());
            System.out.println("[CommentService] 鎻掑叆鍚庢煡璇㈠埌璇勮鏁帮細" + (comments != null ? comments.size() : 0));

            Comment newComment = null;

            // 鍊掑簭鏌ユ壘鏈€鏂扮殑鍖归厤璇勮
            for (int i = comments.size() - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
                Comment c = comments.get(i);
                // 鍏抽敭淇锛氶槻姝㈢┖鎸囬拡锛屼娇鐢∣bjects.equals 鎴栫‘淇濅笉涓簄ull
                if (c != null && c.getUserId() != null && c.getUserId().equals(dto.getUserId())) {
                    // 鍐嶉獙璇佸唴瀹规槸鍚﹀尮閰嶏紙闃叉骞跺彂鏃舵嬁鍒板叾浠栬瘎璁猴級
                    if (dto.getContent().trim().equals(c.getContent())) {
                        newComment = c;
                        System.out.println("[CommentService] 鎵惧埌鍒氭彃鍏ョ殑璇勮锛宨d=" + c.getId());
                        break;
                    }
                }
            }

            // 濡傛灉鎵句笉鍒帮紙鏋佺鎯呭喌锛夛紝鏋勯€犱竴涓繑鍥炲璞★紝閬垮厤500閿欒
            if (newComment == null) {
                System.err.println("[CommentService] 璀﹀憡锛氭湭鑳芥煡璇㈠埌鍒氭彃鍏ョ殑璇勮锛岃繑鍥炴瀯閫犲璞?);
                CommentVO vo = new CommentVO();
                vo.setContent(dto.getContent().trim());
                vo.setUserId(dto.getUserId());
                vo.setUserName(user.getUserName());
                vo.setAvatar(user.getAvatar());
                // 鐢变簬娌℃湁ID锛岃缃负0鎴栫壒娈婂€?
                vo.setCommentId(0);
                vo.setLikeCount(0);
                vo.setHasLiked(false);
                return vo;
            }

            // 缁勮VO
            return convertToVO(newComment, user, dto.getUserId());
        } catch (Exception e) {
            System.err.println("[CommentService] 鍙戝竷璇勮寮傚父: " + e.getMessage());
            e.printStackTrace();
            throw new BusinessException(500, "璇勮鍙戝竷澶辫触: " + e.getMessage());
        }
    }

    @Override
    public boolean deleteComment(Integer commentId, Integer operatorId) {
        if (commentId == null || operatorId == null) {
            throw new BusinessException(400, "鍙傛暟涓嶈兘涓虹┖");
        }

        Comment comment = commentMapper.findById(commentId);
        if (comment == null || comment.getStatus() == 0) {
            throw new BusinessException(404, "璇勮涓嶅瓨鍦ㄦ垨宸插垹闄?);
        }

        User operator = userMapper.findById(operatorId);
        if (operator == null) {
            throw new BusinessException(404, "鎿嶄綔鑰呬笉瀛樺湪");
        }

        // 鏉冮檺鏍￠獙锛氬彧鑳藉垹闄よ嚜宸辩殑璇勮锛屾垨绠＄悊鍛?role=1)
        if (!operatorId.equals(comment.getUserId()) && operator.getRole() != 1) {
            throw new BusinessException(403, "鏃犳潈鍒犻櫎浠栦汉璇勮");
        }

        commentMapper.deleteById(commentId);
        return true;
    }

    @Override
    public List<CommentVO> getCommentsByGoodId(Integer goodId, Integer currentUserId) {
        if (goodId == null) {
            throw new BusinessException(400, "鍟嗗搧ID涓嶈兘涓虹┖");
        }

        List<Comment> comments = commentMapper.findByGoodId(goodId);

        // 鍏抽敭鏂板锛氳缁嗚瘖鏂棩蹇?
        System.out.println("[CommentService] 浠庢暟鎹簱鏌ヨ鍒板師濮嬭瘎璁烘暟: " + (comments != null ? comments.size() : 0) + ", goodId=" + goodId);

        List<CommentVO> voList = new ArrayList<>();

        if (comments == null) {
            System.out.println("[CommentService] 鏌ヨ缁撴灉涓?null");
            return voList;
        }

        for (int i = 0; i < comments.size(); i++) {
            Comment comment = comments.get(i);
            try {
                // 鍏抽敭鏂板锛氭墦鍗板師濮嬭瘎璁哄璞＄殑鎵€鏈夊瓧娈碉紝鐢ㄤ簬璇婃柇
                System.out.println("[CommentService] 绗?" + i + " 鏉¤瘎璁哄師濮嬫暟鎹? id=" + comment.getId()
                        + ", goodId=" + comment.getGoodId()
                        + ", userId=" + comment.getUserId()  // 濡傛灉杩欎釜鍊兼槸 null锛岃鏄?MapperProxy 鏄犲皠澶辫触锛?
                        + ", content=" + (comment.getContent() != null ? comment.getContent().substring(0, Math.min(30, comment.getContent().length())) : "null")
                        + ", status=" + comment.getStatus()
                        + ", createTime=" + comment.getCreateTime());

                if (comment == null) {
                    System.out.println("[CommentService] 绗?" + i + " 鏉¤瘎璁哄璞′负 null锛岃烦杩?);
                    continue;
                }

                // 妫€鏌ヨ瘎璁虹殑 userId 鏄惁瀛樺湪
                if (comment.getUserId() == null) {
                    System.err.println("[CommentService] 涓ラ噸璀﹀憡锛氳瘎璁篒D " + comment.getId() + " 鐨?userId 涓?null锛?);
                    System.err.println("[CommentService] 杩欓€氬父鎰忓懗鐫€ MapperProxy 瀛楁鏄犲皠澶辫触锛堜笅鍒掔嚎杞┘宄板け璐ワ級");
                    continue;
                }

                User user = userMapper.findById(comment.getUserId());
                if (user != null) {
                    CommentVO vo = convertToVO(comment, user, currentUserId);
                    if (vo != null) {
                        voList.add(vo);
                        System.out.println("[CommentService] 绗?" + i + " 鏉¤瘎璁鸿浆鎹㈡垚鍔?);
                    }
                } else {
                    System.err.println("[CommentService] 璀﹀憡锛氭湭鎵惧埌璇勮ID " + comment.getId() + " 瀵瑰簲鐨勭敤鎴? + comment.getUserId());
                }
            } catch (Exception e) {
                System.err.println("[CommentService] 澶勭悊绗?" + i + " 鏉¤瘎璁哄紓甯? " + e.getMessage());
                e.printStackTrace();
                continue;
            }
        }

        System.out.println("[CommentService] 鏈€缁堣繑鍥炲墠绔殑 VO 鏁? " + voList.size());
        return voList;
    }

    // 鍏抽敭淇锛氬鍔?null 妫€鏌?
    private CommentVO convertToVO(Comment comment, User user, Integer currentUserId) {
        if (comment == null || user == null) {
            System.err.println("[CommentService] convertToVO 鏀跺埌 null 鍙傛暟: comment=" + comment + ", user=" + user);
            return null;
        }

        try {
            CommentVO vo = new CommentVO();
            vo.setCommentId(comment.getId());
            vo.setContent(comment.getContent());
            vo.setCreateTime(comment.getCreateTime());
            vo.setUserId(user.getId());
            vo.setUserName(user.getUserName());
            vo.setAvatar(user.getAvatar());

            // 鏌ヨ鐪熷疄鐐硅禐鏁?
            int likeCount = likeMapper.countByCommentId(comment.getId());
            vo.setLikeCount(likeCount);

            // 鏌ヨ褰撳墠鐢ㄦ埛鏄惁宸茬偣璧?
            if (currentUserId != null) {
                boolean hasLiked = likeMapper.findByBothId(comment.getId(), currentUserId) != null;
                vo.setHasLiked(hasLiked);
            } else {
                vo.setHasLiked(false);
            }

            return vo;
        } catch (Exception e) {
            System.err.println("[CommentService] convertToVO 寮傚父: " + e.getMessage());
            e.printStackTrace();
            return null;
        }
    }

    @Override
    public int getCommentCount(Integer goodId) {
        if (goodId == null) {
            return 0;
        }
        return commentMapper.countByGoodId(goodId);
    }

    @Override
    public boolean adminDeleteComment(Integer commentId) {
        if (commentId == null) {
            throw new BusinessException(400, "璇勮ID涓嶈兘涓虹┖");
        }

        Comment comment = commentMapper.findById(commentId);
        if (comment == null || comment.getStatus() == 0) {
            throw new BusinessException(404, "璇勮涓嶅瓨鍦ㄦ垨宸插垹闄?);
        }

        commentMapper.deleteById(commentId);
        return true;
    }
}
