===== FILE: .\vue3\src\views\Home.vue =====

<template>
  <div class="home">
    <!-- 鐜荤拑鎬佸ご閮?-->
    <header class="header glass">
      <div class="header-content">
        <div class="brand">
          <span class="brand-icon">馃悷</span>
          <h1>闂查奔浜ゆ槗骞冲彴</h1>
        </div>

        <div class="user-section" v-if="userInfo && userInfo.userId">
          <div class="user-card" @click="goUserCenter">
            <div class="avatar" v-if="userInfo.avatar">
              <img :src="userInfo.avatar" alt="avatar" @error="handleAvatarError">
            </div>
            <div class="avatar placeholder" v-else>
              {{ userInfo.userName ? userInfo.userName[0].toUpperCase() : '?' }}
            </div>
            <span class="username">{{ userInfo.userName || '鐢ㄦ埛' }}</span>
            <span v-if="isAdmin" class="admin-badge">绠＄悊鍛?/span>
            <span class="arrow">鈫?/span>
          </div>
          <button class="btn-icon" @click="logout" title="閫€鍑?>
            馃毆
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </header>

    <div class="container">
      <!-- 鎼滅储鏍?-->
      <div class="search-section glass">
        <div class="search-box">
          <span class="search-icon">馃攳</span>
          <input
              v-model="searchKeyword"
              type="text"
              placeholder="鎼滅储蹇冧华鐨勫晢鍝?.."
              @keyup.enter="handleSearch"
          >
          <button class="btn-search" @click="handleSearch">
            鎼滅储
          </button>
        </div>
        <button
            v-if="isSearchMode"
            class="btn-reset"
            @click="resetSearch"
        >
          杩斿洖鎺ㄨ崘
        </button>
      </div>

      <!-- 鏍囩绛涢€夋爮 - 鏂板 -->
      <div class="tags-section glass" v-if="!isSearchMode">
        <div class="tags-header">
          <span class="tags-icon">馃彿锔?/span>
          <span class="tags-title">鎸夋爣绛剧瓫閫夛細</span>
          <button
              class="btn-clear-tag"
              v-if="selectedTag"
              @click="clearTagFilter"
          >
            娓呴櫎绛涢€?
          </button>
        </div>
        <div class="tags-list">
          <button
              v-for="tag in tagList"
              :key="tag.tagId"
              class="tag-btn"
              :class="{ 'active': selectedTag === tag.tagId }"
              @click="handleTagSelect(tag.tagId)"
          >
            {{ tag.tagName }}
          </button>
          <div v-if="tagLoading" class="tag-loading">鍔犺浇涓?..</div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <!-- 鏍囬鏍?-->
      <div class="section-header">
        <h2>
          <span class="title-icon">{{ isSearchMode ? '馃攳' : (selectedTag ? '馃彿锔? : '鉁?) }}</span>
          {{ isSearchMode ? '鎼滅储缁撴灉' : (selectedTag ? currentTagName : '绮鹃€夋帹鑽?) }}
        </h2>
        <button class="btn-publish glass" @click="emit('switch-to-publish')">
          <span>+</span>
          鍙戝竷鍟嗗搧
        </button>
      </div>

      <!-- 鍔犺浇鐘舵€?-->
      <div v-if="loading" class="loading-grid">
        <div v-for="i in 8" :key="i" class="skeleton-card glass"></div>
      </div>

      <!-- 绌虹姸鎬?-->
      <div v-else-if="goodsList.length === 0" class="empty-state glass">
        <div class="empty-icon">{{ isSearchMode ? '馃攳' : '馃摝' }}</div>
        <h3>{{ isSearchMode ? '娌℃湁鎵惧埌鐩稿叧鍟嗗搧' : '鏆傛棤鍟嗗搧' }}</h3>
        <p>{{ isSearchMode ? '鎹釜鍏抽敭璇嶈瘯璇? : '鎴愪负绗竴涓彂甯冭€呭惂锛? }}</p>
        <button v-if="!isSearchMode && !selectedTag" class="btn-primary" @click="emit('switch-to-publish')">
          绔嬪嵆鍙戝竷
        </button>
        <button v-if="selectedTag" class="btn-primary" @click="clearTagFilter">
          鏌ョ湅鍏ㄩ儴鍟嗗搧
        </button>
      </div>

      <!-- 鍟嗗搧缃戞牸 -->
      <div v-else class="goods-grid">
        <div
            v-for="(item, index) in goodsList"
            :key="item.id"
            class="goods-card glass card-hover"
            :style="{ animationDelay: `${index * 0.05}s` }"
            @click="viewDetail(item.id)"
        >
          <div class="image-wrapper">
            <div v-if="!item.goodImage || !item.goodImage.trim()" class="no-image glass-dark">
              <span>馃摲</span>
              <p>鏆傛棤鍥剧墖</p>
            </div>
            <img
                v-else
                :src="item.goodImage"
                :alt="item.goodName"
                loading="lazy"
                @error="hideImage($event)"
            >
            <div class="price-tag">
              楼{{ formatPrice(item.goodPrice) }}
            </div>
            <div class="status-badge" :class="item.sellingStatus">
              {{ formatStatus(item.sellingStatus) }}
            </div>
          </div>

          <div class="goods-info">
            <h3 class="title">{{ item.goodName }}</h3>
            <p class="description">{{ item.description || '鏆傛棤鎻忚堪' }}</p>

            <!-- 鍗栧淇℃伅 -->
            <div class="seller-row" v-if="item.sellerInfo || item.sellerId">
              <div class="mini-avatar">
                <img v-if="item.sellerInfo?.avatar" :src="item.sellerInfo.avatar" alt="avatar" @error="$event.target.style.display='none'">
                <span v-else>{{ (item.sellerInfo?.userName || '?')[0] }}</span>
              </div>
              <span class="seller-name">{{ item.sellerInfo?.userName || ('鍗栧' + item.sellerId) }}</span>
            </div>

            <div class="meta">
              <span class="time">鍒氬垰</span>
              <span class="views">馃憖 0</span>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>
</template>

<script setup>
import { ref, onMounted, computed } from 'vue'
import { getRandomGoods, searchGoods, getGoodsByTag } from '@/api/good'
import { getAllTags } from '@/api/tag'

const emit = defineEmits(['switch-to-publish', 'logout', 'view-detail', 'go-user-center'])

const goodsList = ref([])
const loading = ref(true)
const userInfo = ref({})
const searchKeyword = ref('')
const isSearchMode = ref(false)

// 鏍囩鐩稿叧 - 鏂板
const tagList = ref([])
const selectedTag = ref(null)
const tagLoading = ref(false)

// 璁＄畻灞炴€э細绠＄悊鍛樺垽鏂?
const isAdmin = computed(() => {
  return userInfo.value && userInfo.value.role === 1
})

// 璁＄畻褰撳墠閫変腑鐨勬爣绛惧悕绉?
const currentTagName = computed(() => {
  const tag = tagList.value.find(t => t.tagId === selectedTag.value)
  return tag ? tag.tagName : '鏍囩鍟嗗搧'
})

onMounted(() => {
  loadUserInfo()
  fetchGoods()
  fetchTags() // 鍔犺浇鏍囩鍒楄〃
})

const loadUserInfo = () => {
  try {
    const stored = localStorage.getItem('userInfo')
    if (stored) {
      const parsed = JSON.parse(stored)
      userInfo.value = {
        userId: parsed.userId || parsed.id,
        userName: parsed.userName || parsed.username || '鐢ㄦ埛',
        avatar: parsed.avatar || parsed.userAvatar || '',
        role: parsed.role || 0
      }
      console.log('[Home] 鐢ㄦ埛淇℃伅鍔犺浇鎴愬姛:', userInfo.value)
    }
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('[Home] 瑙ｆ瀽鐢ㄦ埛淇℃伅澶辫触:', error)
    userInfo.value = {}
  }
}

// 鑾峰彇鏍囩鍒楄〃
const fetchTags = async () => {
  tagLoading.value = true
  try {
    const res = await getAllTags()
    if (res.code === 200) {
      tagList.value = res.data || []
    }
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('鑾峰彇鏍囩鍒楄〃澶辫触:', error)
  } finally {
    tagLoading.value = false
  }
}

// 澶勭悊鏍囩閫夋嫨
const handleTagSelect = async (tagId) => {
  if (selectedTag.value === tagId) return // 閲嶅鐐瑰嚮涓嶅埛鏂?

  loading.value = true
  selectedTag.value = tagId
  isSearchMode.value = false
  searchKeyword.value = ''

  try {
    const res = await getGoodsByTag(tagId)
    if (res.code === 200) {
      goodsList.value = res.data || []
      // 澶勭悊鍗栧淇℃伅
      goodsList.value.forEach(item => {
        if (!item.sellerInfo && item.sellerId) {
          item.sellerInfo = {
            userName: '鍗栧' + item.sellerId,
            avatar: ''
          }
        }
      })
    } else {
      goodsList.value = []
    }
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('鏍规嵁鏍囩鑾峰彇鍟嗗搧澶辫触:', error)
    goodsList.value = []
  } finally {
    loading.value = false
  }
}

// 娓呴櫎鏍囩绛涢€?
const clearTagFilter = () => {
  selectedTag.value = null
  fetchGoods()
}

const fetchGoods = async () => {
  loading.value = true
  isSearchMode.value = false
  searchKeyword.value = ''
  selectedTag.value = null

  try {
    const res = await getRandomGoods(8)
    if (res.code === 200) {
      goodsList.value = res.data || []
      // 澶勭悊鍗栧淇℃伅
      goodsList.value.forEach(item => {
        if (!item.sellerInfo && item.sellerId) {
          item.sellerInfo = {
            userName: '鍗栧' + item.sellerId,
            avatar: ''
          }
        }
      })
    }
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('鑾峰彇鍟嗗搧澶辫触:', error)
  } finally {
    loading.value = false
  }
}

const handleSearch = async () => {
  if (!searchKeyword.value.trim()) {
    fetchGoods()
    return
  }

  loading.value = true
  isSearchMode.value = true
  selectedTag.value = null // 鎼滅储鏃舵竻闄ゆ爣绛鹃€夋嫨

  try {
    const res = await searchGoods(searchKeyword.value.trim())
    if (res.code === 200) {
      goodsList.value = res.data || []
    } else {
      goodsList.value = []
    }
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('鎼滅储澶辫触:', error)
    goodsList.value = []
  } finally {
    loading.value = false
  }
}

const resetSearch = () => {
  searchKeyword.value = ''
  fetchGoods()
}

const formatPrice = (price) => {
  return price ? parseFloat(price).toFixed(2) : '0.00'
}

const formatStatus = (status) => {
  const map = {
    '鏈嚭鍞?: '鍦ㄥ敭',
    '宸插嚭鍞?: '宸插敭',
    'offline': '涓嬫灦'
  }
  return map[status] || status
}

const viewDetail = (id) => {
  emit('view-detail', id)
}

const hideImage = (e) => {
  const img = e.target
  const src = img.src
  const item = goodsList.value.find(g => g.goodImage === src)
  if (item) {
    item.goodImage = ''
  }
}

const handleAvatarError = () => {
  userInfo.value.avatar = null
}

const goUserCenter = () => {
  emit('go-user-center')
}

const logout = () => {
  localStorage.removeItem('userInfo')
  emit('logout')
}
</script>

  width: 40px;

  border-radius: 50%;

<template>
  <div class="login-container">
    <!-- 瑁呴グ鑳屾櫙鍏冪礌 -->
    <div class="bg-shapes">
      <div class="shape shape-1"></div>
      <div class="shape shape-2"></div>
      <div class="shape shape-3"></div>
    </div>

    <div class="login-box glass">
      <div class="login-header">
        <div class="logo">馃悷</div>
        <h2 class="text-gradient">闂查奔浜ゆ槗骞冲彴</h2>
        <p class="subtitle">瀹夊叏渚挎嵎鐨勪簩鎵嬩氦鏄?/p>
      </div>

      <form @submit.prevent="handleLogin" class="login-form">
        <div class="input-group">
          <div class="input-icon">馃懁</div>
          <input
              v-model="loginForm.userName"
              type="text"
              placeholder="鐢ㄦ埛鍚?
              required
          >
        </div>

        <div class="input-group">
          <div class="input-icon">馃敀</div>
          <input
              v-model="loginForm.password"
              type="password"
              placeholder="瀵嗙爜"
              required
          >
        </div>

        <button
            type="submit"
            class="btn-primary"
            :disabled="loading"
            :class="{ 'loading': loading }"
        >
          <span v-if="!loading">鐧?褰?/span>
          <span v-else class="spinner"></span>
        </button>
      </form>

      <div v-if="errorMsg" class="error-toast">
        <span>鈿狅笍</span> {{ errorMsg }}
      </div>

      <div class="login-footer">
        <div class="divider">
          <span>杩樻病鏈夎处鍙凤紵</span>
        </div>
        <button class="btn-secondary" @click="emit('switch-to-register')">
          绔嬪嵆娉ㄥ唽
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>

    <!-- 搴曢儴瑁呴グ -->
    <div class="floating-icons">
      <span class="float-item" style="--i:1">馃摫</span>
      <span class="float-item" style="--i:2">馃捇</span>
      <span class="float-item" style="--i:3">馃摲</span>
      <span class="float-item" style="--i:4">馃憻</span>
      <span class="float-item" style="--i:5">馃幃</span>
    </div>
  </div>
</template>

<script setup>
import { ref, reactive } from 'vue'
import { login } from '@/api/user'

const emit = defineEmits(['login-success', 'switch-to-register'])

const loginForm = reactive({
  userName: '',
  password: ''
})

const loading = ref(false)
const errorMsg = ref('')

const handleLogin = async () => {
  loading.value = true
  errorMsg.value = ''

  try {
    const res = await login(loginForm)
    if (res.code === 200 && res.data.success) {
      localStorage.setItem('userInfo', JSON.stringify({
        userId: res.data.userId,
        userName: res.data.userName,
        avatar: res.data.avatar,
        role: res.data.role
      }))
      emit('login-success')
    } else {
      errorMsg.value = res.message || '鐧诲綍澶辫触'
    }
  } catch (error) {
    errorMsg.value = error.response?.data?.message || '缃戠粶閿欒'
  } finally {
    loading.value = false
  }
}
</script>

        </div>

        <!-- 鈽呪槄鈽?鏍囩閫夋嫨鍖哄煙锛堟敮鎸佺敤鎴锋坊鍔犳柊鏍囩锛夆槄鈽呪槄 -->
        <div class="form-section">
          <label class="section-label">
            <span class="required">*</span>
            鍟嗗搧鏍囩
            <span class="tag-hint">(鑷冲皯閫夋嫨1涓紝鏈€澶?涓紱鏃犲悎閫傛爣绛惧彲鑷繁娣诲姞)</span>
          </label>

          <!-- 鏍囩鍔犺浇涓?-->
          <div v-if="tagLoading" class="tag-loading">
            <span class="small-spinner"></span> 鍔犺浇鏍囩涓?..
          </div>

          <!-- 宸叉湁鏍囩鍒楄〃 -->
          <div v-else class="tags-container">
            <div class="existing-tags">
              <div class="tags-header-row">
                <span class="sub-label">閫夋嫨宸叉湁鏍囩锛?/span>
                <button
                    type="button"
                    class="btn-add-new"
                    @click="showAddTagInput = true"
                    v-if="!showAddTagInput"
                >
                  <span>+</span> 娣诲姞鏂版爣绛?
                </button>
              </div>

              <div v-if="tagList.length > 0" class="tag-select-area">
                <div
                    v-for="tag in tagList"
                    :key="tag.tagId"
                    class="tag-option"
                    :class="{
                      'selected': selectedTags.includes(tag.tagId),
                      'disabled': selectedTags.length >= 3 && !selectedTags.includes(tag.tagId)
                    }"
                    @click="toggleTag(tag.tagId)"
                >
                  <span class="tag-icon">馃彿锔?/span>
                  {{ tag.tagName }}
                  <span v-if="selectedTags.includes(tag.tagId)" class="check-icon">鉁?/span>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div v-else class="no-tags-hint">
                <p>鏆傛棤宸叉湁鏍囩锛屽揩鏉ュ垱寤虹涓€涓惂锛?/p>
              </div>
            </div>

            <!-- 娣诲姞鏂版爣绛捐緭鍏ュ尯鍩?-->
            <div v-if="showAddTagInput" class="add-tag-section glass-dark">
              <div class="add-tag-header">
                <span>馃彿锔?鍒涘缓鏂版爣绛?/span>
                <button type="button" class="btn-close" @click="showAddTagInput = false">脳</button>
              </div>
              <div class="add-tag-row">
                <input
                    v-model="newTagName"
                    type="text"
                    placeholder="杈撳叆鏍囩鍚嶇О锛屽锛氭暟鐮併€佷功绫嶃€佺編濡?.."
                    maxlength="20"
                    @keyup.enter="handleCreateTag"
                >
                <button
                    type="button"
                    class="btn-create-tag"
                    @click="handleCreateTag"
                    :disabled="!newTagName.trim() || creatingTag"
                >
                  <span v-if="creatingTag" class="mini-spinner"></span>
                  <span v-else>鍒涘缓</span>
                </button>
              </div>
              <p class="add-tag-hint">鎸夊洖杞﹂敭蹇€熷垱寤猴紝鍒涘缓鍚庤嚜鍔ㄩ€変腑</p>
              <p v-if="createTagError" class="error-text">{{ createTagError }}</p>
            </div>

            <!-- 宸查€夋爣绛惧睍绀?-->
            <div v-if="selectedTags.length > 0" class="selected-tags-preview">
              <span class="preview-label">宸查€夋嫨 {{ selectedTags.length }}/3锛?/span>
              <span
                  v-for="tagId in selectedTags"
                  :key="tagId"
                  class="selected-tag"
                  :class="{ 'is-new': isNewTag(tagId) }"
              >
                {{ getTagName(tagId) }}
                <button type="button" class="remove-tag" @click="toggleTag(tagId)">脳</button>
              </span>
            </div>
          </div>

          <p v-if="tagError" class="error-text">{{ tagError }}</p>
        </div>

        <!-- 鍟嗗搧鎻忚堪 -->
        <div class="form-section">
          <label class="section-label">鍟嗗搧鎻忚堪</label>
          <textarea
              v-model="form.description"
              rows="4"
              placeholder="鎻忚堪涓€涓嬪疂璐濈殑鍝佺墝銆佽鏍笺€佹柊鏃х▼搴︺€佸叆鎵嬫笭閬撶瓑..."
              maxlength="500"
          ></textarea>
          <span class="char-count">{{ form.description.length }}/500</span>
        </div>

        <!-- 鍙戝竷鎸夐挳 -->
        <div class="form-actions">
          <button type="button" class="btn-secondary" @click="goBack">
            鍙栨秷
          </button>
          <button
              type="submit"
              class="btn-primary"
              :disabled="submitting || selectedTags.length === 0"
          >
            <span v-if="submitting" class="btn-spinner"></span>
            <span v-else>绔嬪嵆鍙戝竷</span>
          </button>
        </div>
      </form>
    </div>
  </div>
</template>

<script setup>
import { ref, reactive, onMounted } from 'vue'
import { publishGood } from '@/api/good'
import { getAllTags, createTag } from '@/api/tag'
import ImageUpload from '@/components/ImageUpload.vue'
import { ElMessage } from 'element-plus'

const emit = defineEmits(['publish-success', 'go-home'])

const form = reactive({
  goodName: '',
  image: '',
  price: null,
  description: ''
})

const selectedTags = ref([])    // 閫変腑鐨勬爣绛綢D鏁扮粍
const tagList = ref([])         // 鎵€鏈夊凡鏈夋爣绛惧垪琛?
const newTagIds = ref([])       // 璁板綍鏈鏂板垱寤虹殑鏍囩ID锛岀敤浜庢牱寮忓尯鍒?
const tagLoading = ref(false)
const tagError = ref('')
const submitting = ref(false)
const loading = ref(false)

// 娣诲姞鏂版爣绛剧浉鍏?
const showAddTagInput = ref(false)
const newTagName = ref('')
const creatingTag = ref(false)
const createTagError = ref('')

// 鑾峰彇鏍囩鍒楄〃
const fetchTags = async () => {
  tagLoading.value = true
  try {
    const res = await getAllTags()
    if (res.code === 200) {
      tagList.value = res.data || []
    } else {
      tagError.value = '鑾峰彇鏍囩鍒楄〃澶辫触'
    }
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('鑾峰彇鏍囩澶辫触:', error)
    tagError.value = '鑾峰彇鏍囩鍒楄〃澶辫触锛岃鍒锋柊閲嶈瘯'
  } finally {
    tagLoading.value = false
  }
}

onMounted(() => {
  fetchTags()
})

// 鍒囨崲鏍囩閫夋嫨
const toggleTag = (tagId) => {
  const index = selectedTags.value.indexOf(tagId)
  if (index > -1) {
    // 宸查€変腑锛屽垯鍙栨秷
    selectedTags.value.splice(index, 1)
    tagError.value = ''
  } else {
    // 鏈€変腑锛屽垯娣诲姞锛堟渶澶?涓級
    if (selectedTags.value.length >= 3) {
      tagError.value = '鏈€澶氬彧鑳介€夋嫨3涓爣绛?
      return
    }
    selectedTags.value.push(tagId)
    tagError.value = ''
  }
}

// 鑾峰彇鏍囩鍚嶇О
const getTagName = (tagId) => {
  const tag = tagList.value.find(t => t.tagId === tagId)
  return tag ? tag.tagName : ''
}

// 鍒ゆ柇鏄惁鏄柊鍒涘缓鐨勬爣绛?
const isNewTag = (tagId) => {
  return newTagIds.value.includes(tagId)
}

// 鍒涘缓鏂版爣绛?
const handleCreateTag = async () => {
  const name = newTagName.value.trim()
  if (!name) {
    createTagError.value = '璇疯緭鍏ユ爣绛惧悕绉?
    return
  }

  if (name.length > 20) {
    createTagError.value = '鏍囩鍚嶇О涓嶈兘瓒呰繃20涓瓧绗?
    return
  }

  creatingTag.value = true
  createTagError.value = ''

  try {
    const res = await createTag(name)
    if (res.code === 200) {
      const newTag = res.data
      // 娣诲姞鍒版爣绛惧垪琛?
      tagList.value.push(newTag)
      // 鑷姩閫変腑鏂版爣绛?
      selectedTags.value.push(newTag.tagId)
      // 璁板綍涓烘柊鍒涘缓鐨勬爣绛撅紙鐢ㄤ簬鏍峰紡鏍囪锛?
      newTagIds.value.push(newTag.tagId)

      // 娓呯┖杈撳叆妗嗗苟鍏抽棴
      newTagName.value = ''
      showAddTagInput.value = false
      tagError.value = ''

      ElMessage.success('鏍囩鍒涘缓鎴愬姛骞跺凡閫変腑')
    } else {
      createTagError.value = res.message || '鍒涘缓澶辫触'
    }
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('鍒涘缓鏍囩澶辫触:', error)
    createTagError.value = error.response?.data?.message || '鍒涘缓鏍囩澶辫触'
  } finally {
    creatingTag.value = false
  }
}

// 鏍￠獙琛ㄥ崟
const validateForm = () => {
  if (!form.goodName.trim()) {
    ElMessage.warning('璇疯緭鍏ュ晢鍝佸悕绉?)
    return false
  }
  if (!form.price || form.price <= 0) {
    ElMessage.warning('璇疯緭鍏ユ湁鏁堢殑浠锋牸')
    return false
  }
  if (selectedTags.value.length === 0) {
    ElMessage.warning('璇疯嚦灏戦€夋嫨涓€涓晢鍝佹爣绛?)
    tagError.value = '璇疯嚦灏戦€夋嫨涓€涓爣绛?
    return false
  }
  if (selectedTags.value.length > 3) {
    ElMessage.warning('鏈€澶氬彧鑳介€夋嫨3涓爣绛?)
    return false
  }
  return true
}

const handleSubmit = async () => {
  if (!validateForm()) return

  submitting.value = true
  try {
    const res = await publishGood({
      ...form,
      tagIds: selectedTags.value  // 浼犲叆閫変腑鐨勬爣绛撅紙鍖呮嫭鏂板垱寤虹殑锛?
    })

    if (res.code === 200) {
      ElMessage.success('鍙戝竷鎴愬姛锛?)
      emit('publish-success')
    } else {
      ElMessage.error(res.message || '鍙戝竷澶辫触')
    }
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('鍙戝竷澶辫触:', error)
    ElMessage.error(error.response?.data?.message || '鍙戝竷澶辫触锛岃閲嶈瘯')
  } finally {
    submitting.value = false
  }
}

const goBack = () => {
  emit('go-home')
}
</script>

  border: 1px solid rgba(102, 126, 234, 0.3);

<template>
  <div class="register-container">
    <!-- 鑳屾櫙瑁呴グ -->
    <div class="bg-shapes">
      <div class="shape shape-1"></div>
      <div class="shape shape-2"></div>
      <div class="shape shape-3"></div>
    </div>

    <div class="register-box glass">
      <div class="register-header">
        <div class="logo">馃摑</div>
        <h2 class="text-gradient">鍒涘缓璐﹀彿</h2>
        <p class="subtitle">鍔犲叆闂查奔浜ゆ槗骞冲彴</p>
      </div>

      <form @submit.prevent="handleRegister" class="register-form">
        <div class="input-group">
          <div class="input-icon">馃懁</div>
          <input
              v-model="form.userName"
              type="text"
              placeholder="鐢ㄦ埛鍚嶏紙鑷冲皯3浣嶏級"
              required
              minlength="3"
          >
        </div>

        <div class="input-group">
          <div class="input-icon">馃敀</div>
          <input
              v-model="form.password"
              type="password"
              placeholder="瀵嗙爜锛堣嚦灏?浣嶏級"
              required
              minlength="6"
          >
        </div>

        <div class="input-group">
          <div class="input-icon">馃攼</div>
          <input
              v-model="form.confirmPassword"
              type="password"
              placeholder="纭瀵嗙爜"
              required
          >
        </div>

        <!-- 瑙掕壊閫夋嫨鐜荤拑鎷熸€佸崱鐗?-->
        <div class="role-section">
          <label class="section-label">閫夋嫨瑙掕壊</label>
          <div class="role-options">
            <div
                class="role-card"
                :class="{ active: form.role === 0 }"
                @click="form.role = 0"
            >
              <div class="role-icon">馃檵</div>
              <div class="role-info">
                <span class="role-name">鏅€氱敤鎴?/span>
                <span class="role-desc">涔板/鍗栧</span>
              </div>
              <div class="check-mark" v-if="form.role === 0">鉁?/div>
            </div>

            <div
                class="role-card"
                :class="{ active: form.role === 1 }"
                @click="form.role = 1"
            >
              <div class="role-icon">馃懏</div>
              <div class="role-info">
                <span class="role-name">绠＄悊鍛?/span>
                <span class="role-desc">骞冲彴绠＄悊</span>
              </div>
              <div class="check-mark" v-if="form.role === 1">鉁?/div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        <button
            type="submit"
            class="btn-primary"
            :disabled="loading"
        >
          <span v-if="!loading">绔嬪嵆娉ㄥ唽</span>
          <span v-else class="spinner"></span>
        </button>
      </form>

      <div v-if="errorMsg" class="error-toast">
        <span>鈿狅笍</span> {{ errorMsg }}
      </div>

      <div v-if="successMsg" class="success-toast">
        <span>鉁?/span> {{ successMsg }}
      </div>

      <div class="register-footer">
        <div class="divider">
          <span>宸叉湁璐﹀彿锛?/span>
        </div>
        <button class="btn-secondary" @click="emit('switch-to-login')">
          鍘荤櫥褰?
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>
</template>

<script setup>
import { ref, reactive } from 'vue'
import { register } from '@/api/user'

const emit = defineEmits(['register-success', 'switch-to-login'])

const form = reactive({
  userName: '',
  password: '',
  confirmPassword: '',
  role: 0
})

const loading = ref(false)
const errorMsg = ref('')
const successMsg = ref('')

const handleRegister = async () => {
  // 琛ㄥ崟楠岃瘉
  if (form.password !== form.confirmPassword) {
    errorMsg.value = '涓ゆ瀵嗙爜涓嶄竴鑷?
    return
  }

  if (form.userName.length < 3) {
    errorMsg.value = '鐢ㄦ埛鍚嶈嚦灏?浣?
    return
  }

  if (form.password.length < 6) {
    errorMsg.value = '瀵嗙爜鑷冲皯6浣?
    return
  }

  loading.value = true
  errorMsg.value = ''
  successMsg.value = ''

  try {
    const res = await register({
      userName: form.userName,
      password: form.password,
      confirmPassword: form.confirmPassword,
      role: form.role
    })

    if (res.code === 200 && res.data.success) {
      const roleText = form.role === 1 ? '绠＄悊鍛? : '鏅€氱敤鎴?
      successMsg.value = `娉ㄥ唽鎴愬姛锛佽韩浠斤細${roleText}`

      setTimeout(() => {
        alert(`娉ㄥ唽鎴愬姛锛乗n鐢ㄦ埛鍚嶏細${form.userName}\n韬唤锛?{roleText}`)
        emit('register-success')
      }, 500)
    } else {
      errorMsg.value = res.message || '娉ㄥ唽澶辫触'
    }
  } catch (error) {
    errorMsg.value = error.response?.data?.message || '娉ㄥ唽澶辫触锛岃妫€鏌ョ綉缁?
  } finally {
    loading.value = false
  }
}
</script>

            <div v-for="item in favorites" :key="item.good?.id" class="good-card" @click="viewGood(item.good?.id)">
              <div class="good-image">
                <img v-if="item.good?.goodImage" :src="item.good.goodImage" alt="鍟嗗搧">
                <div v-else class="no-image">鏆傛棤鍥剧墖</div>
              </div>
              <div class="good-info">
                <h4 class="good-title">{{ item.good?.goodName || '鏈煡鍟嗗搧' }}</h4>
                <p class="good-price">楼{{ formatPrice(item.good?.goodPrice) }}</p>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        <!-- 鎴戠殑鍏虫敞 -->
        <div v-if="activeTab === 'following'" class="tab-content">
          <div class="section-header">
            <h3>鎴戠殑鍏虫敞</h3>
          </div>
          <div v-if="followings.length === 0" class="empty-state">
            <div class="empty-icon emoji-medium">馃懃</div>
            <p>杩樻病鏈夊叧娉ㄤ换浣曠敤鎴?/p>
          </div>
          <div v-else class="user-list">
            <div v-for="item in followings" :key="item.followingUser?.id" class="user-item">
              <div class="user-avatar-small">
                <img v-if="item.followingUser?.avatar" :src="item.followingUser.avatar">
                <span v-else>{{ item.followingUser?.userName?.[0] || '?' }}</span>
              </div>
              <span class="user-name">{{ item.followingUser?.userName || '鏈煡鐢ㄦ埛' }}</span>
              <button class="btn-unfollow" @click="cancelFollow(item.followingUser?.id)">鍙栨秷鍏虫敞</button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        <!-- 鎴戠殑璁㈠崟 -->
        <div v-if="activeTab === 'orders'" class="tab-content">
          <div class="section-header">
            <h3>鎴戠殑璁㈠崟</h3>
            <div class="order-filters">
              <button :class="{ active: orderType === 'buy' }" @click="orderType = 'buy'; fetchOrders()">璐拱鐨?/button>
              <button :class="{ active: orderType === 'sell' }" @click="orderType = 'sell'; fetchOrders()">鍑哄敭鐨?/button>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div v-if="orders.length === 0" class="empty-state">
            <div class="empty-icon emoji-medium">馃搵</div>
            <p>鏆傛棤璁㈠崟璁板綍</p>
          </div>
          <div v-else class="order-list">
            <div v-for="order in orders" :key="order.orderId" class="order-item glass-dark">
              <div class="order-header">
                <span class="order-id">璁㈠崟 #{{ order.orderId }}</span>
                <span class="order-status" :class="order.status">{{ order.statusName }}</span>
              </div>
              <div class="order-body">
                <div class="order-good">
                  <img v-if="order.goodImage" :src="order.goodImage" class="order-thumb">
                  <div class="order-info">
                    <p class="order-good-name">{{ order.goodName }}</p>
                    <p class="order-price">楼{{ formatPrice(order.price) }}</p>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
              <div class="order-footer">
                <span class="order-time">{{ formatTime(order.createTime) }}</span>
                <div class="order-actions">
                  <button
                      v-if="orderType === 'sell' && order.status === 0"
                      class="btn-confirm"
                      @click="handleConfirmOrder(order.orderId)"
                  >
                    纭鏀舵
                  </button>
                  <button
                      v-if="order.status === 0"
                      class="btn-cancel"
                      @click="handleCancelOrder(order.orderId)"
                  >
                    鍙栨秷璁㈠崟
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>

    <!-- 鍏呭€煎璇濇 -->
    <div v-if="showRecharge" class="dialog-overlay" @click.self="showRecharge = false">
      <div class="dialog glass">
        <h3>馃拵 閽卞寘鍏呭€?/h3>
        <p class="dialog-hint">褰撳墠浣欓: 楼{{ formatBalance(walletBalance) }}</p>
        <div class="amount-options">
          <button
              v-for="amount in rechargeAmounts"
              :key="amount"
              :class="['amount-btn', { active: rechargeAmount === amount }]"
              @click="rechargeAmount = amount"
          >
            楼{{ amount }}
          </button>
        </div>
        <div class="custom-amount">
          <label>鑷畾涔夐噾棰?</label>
          <input
              type="number"
              v-model.number="customRechargeAmount"
              placeholder="杈撳叆閲戦"
              min="1"
              max="10000"
          >
        </div>
        <div class="dialog-actions">
          <button class="btn-secondary" @click="showRecharge = false">鍙栨秷</button>
          <button
              class="btn-primary"
              @click="handleRecharge"
              :disabled="rechargeLoading || (!rechargeAmount && !customRechargeAmount)"
          >
            <span v-if="!rechargeLoading">纭鍏呭€?/span>
            <span v-else class="spinner-small"></span>
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>
</template>

<script setup>
import { ref, computed, onMounted } from 'vue'
import { ElMessage, ElMessageBox } from 'element-plus'
import { getUserProfile, updateAvatar } from '@/api/user'
import { getMyGoods } from '@/api/good'
import { getMyFavorites } from '@/api/favorite'
import { getMyFollowing } from '@/api/follow'
// 鍏抽敭淇锛氱粰瀵煎叆鐨凙PI鍑芥暟璧峰埆鍚嶏紝閬垮厤涓庢湰鍦板畾涔夌殑鍑芥暟鍚嶅啿绐?
import { getMyOrders, confirmOrder as apiConfirmOrder, cancelOrder as apiCancelOrder } from '@/api/order'
import { getWalletBalance, rechargeWallet } from '@/api/wallet'
import request from '@/utils/request'

const emit = defineEmits(['go-home', 'logout', 'view-good', 'go-publish'])

// 鐢ㄦ埛淇℃伅
const userInfo = ref({})
const isAdmin = computed(() => userInfo.value?.role === 1)
const userNameFirstChar = computed(() => {
  return (userInfo.value?.userName || '?')[0].toUpperCase()
})

// 鏍囩椤?
const activeTab = ref('goods')
const tabs = [
  { key: 'goods', label: '鎴戠殑鍙戝竷', icon: '馃摝' },
  { key: 'favorites', label: '鎴戠殑鏀惰棌', icon: '猸? },
  { key: 'following', label: '鎴戠殑鍏虫敞', icon: '馃懃' },
  { key: 'orders', label: '鎴戠殑璁㈠崟', icon: '馃搵' }
]

// 鏁版嵁鍒楄〃
const myGoods = ref([])
const favorites = ref([])
const followings = ref([])
const orders = ref([])
const orderType = ref('buy')

// 閽卞寘鐩稿叧
const walletBalance = ref(0)
const walletLoading = ref(false)
const showRecharge = ref(false)
const rechargeAmount = ref(100)
const customRechargeAmount = ref('')
const rechargeLoading = ref(false)
const rechargeAmounts = [10, 50, 100, 200, 500]

// 澶村儚涓婁紶
const avatarInput = ref(null)

onMounted(() => {
  // 浠巐ocalStorage鑾峰彇鐢ㄦ埛淇℃伅锛堢敤浜庡揩閫熸樉绀猴級
  const stored = localStorage.getItem('userInfo')
  if (stored) {
    userInfo.value = JSON.parse(stored)
  }

  // 鍔犺浇璇︾粏鏁版嵁
  fetchUserProfile()
  fetchWallet()
  fetchMyGoods()
  fetchFavorites()
  fetchFollowing()
  fetchOrders()
})

// 鑾峰彇鐢ㄦ埛璇︽儏
const fetchUserProfile = async () => {
  try {
    const res = await getUserProfile()
    if (res.code === 200) {
      userInfo.value = res.data
      // 鏇存柊localStorage
      localStorage.setItem('userInfo', JSON.stringify(res.data))
    }
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('鑾峰彇鐢ㄦ埛淇℃伅澶辫触:', error)
  }
}

// 鑾峰彇閽卞寘浣欓
const fetchWallet = async () => {
  walletLoading.value = true
  try {
    const res = await getWalletBalance()
    if (res.code === 200) {
      walletBalance.value = res.data?.balance || 0
    }
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('鑾峰彇閽卞寘澶辫触:', error)
  } finally {
    walletLoading.value = false
  }
}

const refreshWallet = () => {
  fetchWallet()
  ElMessage.success('浣欓宸插埛鏂?)
}

// 鍏呭€煎姛鑳?
const showRechargeDialog = () => {
  showRecharge.value = true
  rechargeAmount.value = 100
  customRechargeAmount.value = ''
}

const handleRecharge = async () => {
  const amount = customRechargeAmount.value || rechargeAmount.value
  if (!amount || amount <= 0) {
    ElMessage.warning('璇疯緭鍏ユ湁鏁堥噾棰?)
    return
  }

  if (amount > 10000) {
    ElMessage.warning('鍗曟鍏呭€间笉鑳借秴杩?0000鍏?)
    return
  }

  rechargeLoading.value = true
  try {
    const res = await rechargeWallet(amount)
    if (res.code === 200) {
      ElMessage.success(`鎴愬姛鍏呭€?楼${amount}`)
      showRecharge.value = false
      await fetchWallet() // 鍒锋柊浣欓
    } else {
      ElMessage.error(res.message || '鍏呭€煎け璐?)
    }
  } catch (error) {
    ElMessage.error(error.response?.data?.message || '鍏呭€煎け璐ワ紝璇烽噸璇?)
  } finally {
    rechargeLoading.value = false
  }
}

// 鑾峰彇鎴戠殑鍙戝竷
const fetchMyGoods = async () => {
  try {
    const res = await getMyGoods()
    if (res.code === 200) {
      myGoods.value = res.data?.goods || []
    }
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('鑾峰彇鎴戠殑鍟嗗搧澶辫触:', error)
  }
}

// 鑾峰彇鏀惰棌
const fetchFavorites = async () => {
  try {
    const res = await getMyFavorites()
    if (res.code === 200) {
      favorites.value = res.data?.favorites || []
    }
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('鑾峰彇鏀惰棌澶辫触:', error)
  }
}

// 鑾峰彇鍏虫敞
const fetchFollowing = async () => {
  try {
    const res = await getMyFollowing()
    if (res.code === 200) {
      followings.value = res.data?.followings || []
    }
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('鑾峰彇鍏虫敞澶辫触:', error)
  }
}

// 鑾峰彇璁㈠崟
const fetchOrders = async () => {
  try {
    const res = await getMyOrders(orderType.value)
    if (res.code === 200) {
      orders.value = res.data || []
    }
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('鑾峰彇璁㈠崟澶辫触:', error)
  }
}

// 鍏抽敭淇锛氶噸鍛藉悕鏈湴鍑芥暟锛岄伩鍏嶄笌瀵煎叆鐨凙PI鍑芥暟鍚嶅啿绐?
const handleConfirmOrder = async (orderId) => {
  try {
    await ElMessageBox.confirm('纭宸叉敹鍒版椤癸紵纭鍚庝氦鏄撳皢瀹屾垚銆?, '纭鏀舵', {
      confirmButtonText: '纭',
      cancelButtonText: '鍙栨秷',
      type: 'warning'
    })

    // 浣跨敤鍒悕璋冪敤API
    const res = await apiConfirmOrder(orderId)
    if (res.code === 200) {
      ElMessage.success('纭鎴愬姛')
      fetchOrders()
      fetchWallet() // 鍒锋柊浣欓锛堝崠瀹舵敹娆撅級
    }
  } catch (error) {
    if (error !== 'cancel') {
      ElMessage.error(error.response?.data?.message || '鎿嶄綔澶辫触')
    }
  }
}

// 鍏抽敭淇锛氶噸鍛藉悕鏈湴鍑芥暟锛岄伩鍏嶄笌瀵煎叆鐨凙PI鍑芥暟鍚嶅啿绐?
const handleCancelOrder = async (orderId) => {
  try {
    await ElMessageBox.confirm('纭畾瑕佸彇娑堟璁㈠崟鍚楋紵', '鍙栨秷璁㈠崟', {
      confirmButtonText: '纭畾',
      cancelButtonText: '鍐嶆兂鎯?,
      type: 'warning'
    })

    // 浣跨敤鍒悕璋冪敤API
    const res = await apiCancelOrder(orderId)
    if (res.code === 200) {
      ElMessage.success('璁㈠崟宸插彇娑?)
      fetchOrders()
      fetchWallet() // 鍒锋柊浣欓锛堥€€娆撅級
    }
  } catch (error) {
    if (error !== 'cancel') {
      ElMessage.error(error.response?.data?.message || '鎿嶄綔澶辫触')
    }
  }
}

// 鍙栨秷鍏虫敞
const cancelFollow = async (userId) => {
  if (!userId) return
  try {
    const res = await request({
      url: '/follow/cancel',
      method: 'post',
      data: { followingId: userId }
    })
    if (res.code === 200) {
      ElMessage.success('宸插彇娑堝叧娉?)
      fetchFollowing()
    }
  } catch (error) {
    ElMessage.error('鎿嶄綔澶辫触')
  }
}

// 澶村儚涓婁紶
const triggerAvatarUpload = () => {
  avatarInput.value?.click()
}

const handleAvatarChange = async (e) => {
  const file = e.target.files[0]
  if (!file) return

  if (!file.type.startsWith('image/')) {
    ElMessage.warning('璇烽€夋嫨鍥剧墖鏂囦欢')
    return
  }

  if (file.size > 5 * 1024 * 1024) {
    ElMessage.warning('鍥剧墖澶у皬涓嶈兘瓒呰繃5MB')
    return
  }

  try {
    const formData = new FormData()
    formData.append('file', file)

    const uploadRes = await request({
      url: '/upload/image',
      method: 'post',
      data: formData,
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'multipart/form-data' }
    })

    if (uploadRes.code === 200 && uploadRes.data?.url) {
      // 鏇存柊澶村儚
      const updateRes = await updateAvatar(uploadRes.data.url)
      if (updateRes.code === 200) {
        userInfo.value.avatar = uploadRes.data.url
        localStorage.setItem('userInfo', JSON.stringify(userInfo.value))
        ElMessage.success('澶村儚鏇存柊鎴愬姛')
      }
    }
  } catch (error) {
    ElMessage.error('涓婁紶澶辫触')
  }
  e.target.value = ''
}

// 閫€鍑虹櫥褰?- 淇鍥炬爣杩囧ぇ闂
const handleLogout = () => {
  ElMessageBox.confirm('纭畾瑕侀€€鍑虹櫥褰曞悧锛?, '閫€鍑虹‘璁?, {
    confirmButtonText: '纭畾閫€鍑?,
    cancelButtonText: '鍙栨秷',
    type: 'warning',
    customClass: 'logout-confirm-dialog'
  }).then(() => {
    localStorage.removeItem('userInfo')
    emit('logout')
    ElMessage.success('宸查€€鍑虹櫥褰?)
  }).catch(() => {})
}

// 瀵艰埅
const goHome = () => emit('go-home')
const goPublish = () => emit('go-publish')
const viewGood = (id) => {
  if (id) emit('view-good', id)
}

// 鏍煎紡鍖?
const formatPrice = (price) => {
  return price ? parseFloat(price).toFixed(2) : '0.00'
}

const formatBalance = (balance) => {
  if (!balance) return '0.00'
  return parseFloat(balance).toFixed(2)
}

const formatStatus = (status) => {
  const map = {
    '鏈嚭鍞?: '鍦ㄥ敭',
    '宸插嚭鍞?: '宸插敭',
    'selling': '鍦ㄥ敭',
    'sold': '宸插敭'
  }
  return map[status] || status || '鏈煡'
}

const formatTime = (time) => {
  if (!time) return '-'
  if (typeof time === 'string') {
    return time.replace('T', ' ').substring(0, 16)
  }
  return '-'
}
</script>

<style scoped>

  min-height: 100vh;
  background: var(--gradient-bg, linear-gradient(135deg, #e0c3fc 0%, #8ec5fc 100%));
